## Canada Business Corporations Act {#canada-business-corporations-act .Title-of-Act}

[R.S.C.]{.abbr title="Revised Statutes of Canada"}, 1985, c. C-44
:::

An Act respecting Canadian business corporations
::::

:::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
## [Short Title]{.HTitleText1} {#h-108365 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Short title

**[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** This Act may be cited as
the [Canada Business Corporations Act](/eng/acts/C-44).

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 1
- 1994, c. 24, s. 1(F)
:::

## [PART I]{.HLabel1}[Interpretation and Application]{.HTitleText1} {#h-108370 .Part}

### [Interpretation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-108371 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In this
  Act,

  [[affairs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[affairs]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the relationships among a
      corporation, its affiliates and the shareholders, directors and
      officers of such bodies corporate but does not include the
      business carried on by such bodies corporate; ([affaires
      internes]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[affiliate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[affiliate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an affiliated body
      corporate within the meaning of subsection
      (2); ([groupe]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[articles]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[articles]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the original or restated
      articles of incorporation, articles of amendment, articles of
      amalgamation, articles of continuance, articles of reorganization,
      articles of arrangement, articles of dissolution, articles of
      revival and includes any amendments
      thereto; ([statuts]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[associate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[associate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of a relationship
      with a person, means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a body corporate of which that person
        beneficially owns or controls, directly or indirectly, shares or
        securities currently convertible into shares carrying more than
        ten per cent of the voting rights under all circumstances or by
        reason of the occurrence of an event that has occurred and is
        continuing, or a currently exercisable option or right to
        purchase such shares or such convertible securities,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a partner of that person acting on behalf of
        the partnership of which they are partners,

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a trust or estate or succession in which that
        person has a substantial beneficial interest or in respect of
        which that person serves as a trustee or liquidator of the
        succession or in a similar capacity,

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a spouse of that person or an individual who is
        cohabiting with that person in a conjugal relationship, having
        so cohabited for a period of at least one year,

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a child of that person or of the spouse or
        individual referred to in paragraph (d), and

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a relative of that person or of the spouse or
        individual referred to in paragraph (d), if that relative has
        the same residence as that person; ([liens]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[auditor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[auditor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a partnership of auditors
      or an auditor that is
      incorporated; ([vérificateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[beneficial interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[beneficial interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an interest
      arising out of the beneficial ownership of securities; ([véritable
      propriétaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} et [propriété
      effective]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[beneficial ownership]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[beneficial ownership]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes ownership
      through any trustee, legal representative, agent or mandatary, or
      other intermediary; ([véritable propriétaire]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"} et [propriété effective]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[body corporate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[body corporate]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a company or other
      body corporate wherever or however incorporated; ([personne
      morale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[call]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[call]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an option transferable by
      delivery to demand delivery of a specified number or amount of
      securities at a fixed price within a specified time but does not
      include an option or right to acquire securities of the
      corporation that granted the option or right to acquire; ([option
      d'achat]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a body corporate
      incorporated or continued under this Act and not discontinued
      under this Act; ([société par actions]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"}
      ou [société]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[court]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[court]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the Province of Newfoundland and Labrador,
        the Trial Division of the Supreme Court of the Province,

      - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} in the Province of Ontario, the Superior
        Court of Justice,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the Provinces of Nova Scotia, British
        Columbia and Prince Edward Island, the Supreme Court of the
        Province,

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the Provinces of Manitoba, Saskatchewan,
        Alberta and New Brunswick, the Court of Queen's Bench for the
        Province,

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the Province of Quebec, the Superior Court
        of the Province, and

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the Supreme Court of Yukon, the Supreme Court
        of the Northwest Territories and the Nunavut Court of
        Justice; ([tribunal]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[court of appeal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[court of appeal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the court to which
      an appeal lies from an order of a court; ([Cour
      d'appel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[debt obligation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[debt obligation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a bond, debenture,
      note or other evidence of indebtedness or guarantee of a
      corporation, whether secured or unsecured; ([titre de
      créance]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Director]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Director]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Director appointed
      under section 260; ([directeur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[director]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[director]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person occupying the
      position of director by whatever name called and
      [[directors]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} and [[board of
      directors]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a single
      director; ([administrateur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} et
      [conseil d'administration]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[distributing corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[distributing corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, subject to
      subsections (6) and (7), a distributing corporation as defined in
      the regulations; ([société ayant fait appel au
      public]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a body corporate, a
      partnership, a trust, a joint venture or an unincorporated
      association or organization; ([entité]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[going-private transaction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[going-private transaction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      going-private transaction as defined in the
      regulations; ([opération de fermeture]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[incapable]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[incapable]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an individual,
      means that the individual is found, under the laws of a province,
      to be unable, other than by reason of minority, to manage their
      property or is declared to be incapable by any court in a
      jurisdiction outside Canada; ([incapable]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[incorporator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[incorporator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who signs
      articles of incorporation; ([fondateur]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[individual]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[individual]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a natural
      person; ([particulier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[liability]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[liability]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a debt of a corporation
      arising under section 40, subsection 190(25) and paragraphs
      241(3)(f) and (g); ([passif]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[mandatary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[mandatary]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in Quebec, includes a
      successor; ([mandataire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Minister]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means such member of the Queen's
      Privy Council for Canada as is designated by the Governor in
      Council as the Minister for the purposes of this
      Act; ([ministre]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual appointed as
      an officer under section 121, the chairperson of the board of
      directors, the president, a vice-president, the secretary, the
      treasurer, the comptroller, the general counsel, the general
      manager, a managing director, of a corporation, or any other
      individual who performs functions for a corporation similar to
      those normally performed by an individual occupying any of those
      offices; ([dirigeant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[ordinary resolution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ordinary resolution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a resolution
      passed by a majority of the votes cast by the shareholders who
      voted in respect of that resolution; ([résolution
      ordinaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[person]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual, partnership,
      association, body corporate, or personal
      representative; ([personne]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[personal representative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[personal representative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who
      stands in place of and represents another person including, but
      not limited to, a trustee, an executor, an administrator, a
      liquidator of a succession, an administrator of the property of
      others, a guardian or tutor, a curator, a receiver or
      sequestrator, an agent or mandatary or an attorney; ([représentant
      personnel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[prescribed]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means prescribed by the
      regulations; ([prescrit]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"} ou
      [réglementaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[prior legislation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[prior legislation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the various Acts
      of Parliament that were in force prior to the coming into force of
      this Act and that applied to the incorporation of federal
      companies under those Acts, other than any [[financial
      institution]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in section 2 of the
      [Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01); ([législation
      antérieure]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[put]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[put]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an option transferable by
      delivery to deliver a specified number or amount of securities at
      a fixed price within a specified time; ([option de
      vente]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[redeemable share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[redeemable share]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a share issued by a
      corporation

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that the corporation may purchase or redeem on
        the demand of the corporation, or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that the corporation is required by its
        articles to purchase or redeem at a specified time or on the
        demand of a shareholder; ([action rachetable]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[resident Canadian]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[resident Canadian]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an individual who
      is

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a Canadian citizen ordinarily resident in
        Canada,

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a Canadian citizen not ordinarily resident in
        Canada who is a member of a prescribed class of persons, or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a permanent resident within the meaning of
        subsection 2(1) of the [Immigration and Refugee Protection
        Act](/eng/acts/I-2.5) and ordinarily resident in Canada, except
        a permanent resident who has been ordinarily resident in Canada
        for more than one year after the time at which he or she first
        became eligible to apply for Canadian citizenship; ([résident
        canadien]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[security]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[security]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a share of any class or
      series of shares or a debt obligation of a corporation and
      includes a certificate evidencing such a share or debt
      obligation; ([valeur mobilière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[security interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[security interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an interest or
      right in or charge on property of a corporation to secure payment
      of a debt or performance of any other obligation of the
      corporation; ([sûreté]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[send]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[send]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes
      deliver; ([envoyer]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[series]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[series]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to shares, means a
      division of a class of shares; ([série]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[special resolution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[special resolution]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a resolution
      passed by a majority of not less than two-thirds of the votes cast
      by the shareholders who voted in respect of that resolution or
      signed by all the shareholders entitled to vote on that
      resolution; ([résolution spéciale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[squeeze-out transaction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[squeeze-out transaction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      transaction by a corporation that is not a distributing
      corporation that would require an amendment to its articles and
      would, directly or indirectly, result in the interest of a holder
      of shares of a class of the corporation being terminated without
      the consent of the holder, and without substituting an interest of
      equivalent value in shares issued by the corporation, which shares
      have equal or greater rights and privileges than the shares of the
      affected class; ([opération d'éviction]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[unanimous shareholder agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[unanimous shareholder agreement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
      agreement described in subsection 146(1) or a declaration of a
      shareholder described in subsection 146(2). ([convention unanime
      des actionnaires]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Affiliated bodies corporate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} one body corporate is affiliated with another body
    corporate if one of them is the subsidiary of the other or both are
    subsidiaries of the same body corporate or each of them is
    controlled by the same person; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if two bodies corporate are affiliated with the
    same body corporate at the same time, they are deemed to be
    affiliated with each other.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Control

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, a body corporate is
  controlled by a person or by two or more bodies corporate if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} securities of the body corporate to which are
    attached more than fifty per cent of the votes that may be cast to
    elect directors of the body corporate are held, other than by way of
    security only, by or for the benefit of that person or by or for the
    benefit of those bodies corporate; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the votes attached to those securities are
    sufficient, if exercised, to elect a majority of the directors of
    the body corporate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Holding body corporate

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A body corporate is the holding body corporate of
  another if that other body corporate is its subsidiary.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subsidiary body corporate

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A body corporate is a subsidiary of another body
  corporate if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it is controlled by

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} that other body corporate,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} that other body corporate and one or more bodies
      corporate each of which is controlled by that other body
      corporate, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} two or more bodies corporate each of which is
      controlled by that other body corporate; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it is a subsidiary of a body corporate that is a
    subsidiary of that other body corporate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemptions --- on application by
  corporation

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} On the application of a corporation, the Director may
  determine that the corporation is not or was not a distributing
  corporation if the Director is satisfied that the determination would
  not be prejudicial to the public interest.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemptions --- classes of corporations

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The Director may determine that a class of
  corporations are not or were not distributing corporations if the
  Director is satisfied that the determination would not be prejudicial
  to the public interest.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Infants

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this Act, the word
  [[infant]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} has the same meaning as in the
  applicable provincial law and, in the absence of any such law, has the
  same meaning as the word [[child]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in the United
  Nations Convention on the Rights of the Child, adopted in the United
  Nations General Assembly on November 20, 1989.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 2
- R.S., 1985, c. 27 (2nd Supp.), s. 10

:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Individual with significant control

- **[[2.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of this Act, any of the following individuals is an
  individual with significant control over a corporation:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an individual who has any of the following
    interests or rights, or any combination of them, in respect of a
    significant number of shares of the corporation:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the individual is the registered holder of them,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the individual is the beneficial owner of them,
      or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the individual has direct or indirect control
      or direction over them;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an individual who has any direct or indirect
    influence that, if exercised, would result in control in fact of the
    corporation; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an individual to whom prescribed circumstances
    apply.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Joint ownership or control

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Two or more individuals are each considered to be an
  individual with significant control over a corporation if, in respect
  of a significant number of shares of the corporation,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an interest or right, or a combination of interests
    or rights, referred to in paragraph (1)(a) is held jointly by those
    individuals; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a right, or combination of rights, referred to in
    paragraph (1)(a) is subject to any agreement or arrangement under
    which the right or rights are to be exercised jointly or in concert
    by those individuals.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Significant number of shares

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, a significant
  number of shares of a corporation is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any number of shares that carry 25% or more of the
    voting rights attached to all of the corporation's outstanding
    voting shares; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any number of shares that is equal to 25% or more
    of all of the corporation's outstanding shares measured by fair
    market value.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 27, s. 182](#1039118-1144049){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039118-1144049.html"
  role="button"}
:::

### [Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-108457 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of Act

- **[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This Act
  applies to every corporation incorporated and every body corporate
  continued as a corporation under this Act that has not been
  discontinued under this Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 1991, c. 45, s. 551\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certain Acts do not apply

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The following do not apply to a corporation:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2009, c. 23, s. 344\]]{.Repealed}

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} the [Canada Not-for-profit Corporations
    Act](/eng/acts/C-7.75);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the [Winding-up and Restructuring
    Act](/eng/acts/W-11); and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the provisions of a Special Act, as defined in
    section 87 of the [Canada Transportation Act](/eng/acts/C-10.4),
    that are inconsistent with this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitations on business that may be
  carried on

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} No corporation shall carry on the business of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a bank;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} an association to which the [Cooperative Credit
    Associations Act](/eng/acts/C-41.01) applies;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a company or society to which the [Insurance
    Companies Act](/eng/acts/I-11.8) applies; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a company to which the [Trust and Loan Companies
    Act](/eng/acts/T-19.8) applies.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitations on business that may be
  carried on

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} No corporation shall carry on business as a
  degree-granting educational institution unless expressly authorized to
  do so by a federal or provincial agent that by law has the power to
  confer degree-granting authority on an educational institution.

::: HistoricalNote

- 2009, c. 23, ss. 309, 344

### [Purposes of Act]{.HTitleText2} {#h-108478 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Purposes

**[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** The purposes of this Act
are to revise and reform the law applicable to business corporations
incorporated to carry on business throughout Canada, to advance the
cause of uniformity of business corporation law in Canada and to provide
a means of allowing an orderly transferance of certain federal companies
incorporated under various Acts of Parliament to this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1974-75-76, c. 33, s. 4
- 1978-79, c. 9, s. 1(F)
:::

## [PART II]{.HLabel1}[Incorporation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-108483 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incorporators

- **[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} One or
  more individuals or bodies corporate may incorporate a corporation by
  signing articles of incorporation and complying with section 7.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Individuals

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An individual may incorporate a corporation only if
  that individual

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is not less than 18 years of age;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is not incapable; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} does not have the status of bankrupt.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 5
- 2018, c. 8, s. 2
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-5-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 5"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Articles of incorporation

- **[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Articles
  of incorporation shall follow the form that the Director fixes and
  shall set out, in respect of the proposed corporation,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the corporation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the province in Canada where the registered office
    is to be situated;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the classes and any maximum number of shares that
    the corporation is authorized to issue, and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if there will be two or more classes of shares,
      the rights, privileges, restrictions and conditions attaching to
      each class of shares, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if a class of shares may be issued in series,
      the authority given to the directors to fix the number of shares
      in, and to determine the designation of, and the rights,
      privileges, restrictions and conditions attaching to, the shares
      of each series;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the issue, transfer or ownership of shares of
    the corporation is to be restricted, a statement to that effect and
    a statement as to the nature of such restrictions;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the number of directors or, subject to paragraph
    107(a), the minimum and maximum number of directors of the
    corporation; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} any restrictions on the businesses that the
    corporation may carry on.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional provisions in articles

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The articles may set out any provisions permitted by
  this Act or by law to be set out in the by-laws of the corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special majorities

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), if the articles or a
  unanimous shareholder agreement require a greater number of votes of
  directors or shareholders than that required by this Act to effect any
  action, the provisions of the articles or of the unanimous shareholder
  agreement prevail.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The articles may not require a greater number of
  votes of shareholders to remove a director than the number required by
  section 109.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 6
- 1994, c. 24, s. 4(F)
- 2001, c. 14, ss. 3, 134(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Delivery of articles of incorporation

**[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** An incorporator shall send
to the Director articles of incorporation and the documents required by
sections 19 and 106.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1974-75-76, c. 33, s. 7
- 1978-79, c. 9, s. 1(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of incorporation

- **[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject
  to subsection (2), on receipt of articles of incorporation, the
  Director shall issue a certificate of incorporation in accordance with
  section 262.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- failure to comply with
  Act

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Director may refuse to issue the certificate if a
  notice that is required to be sent under subsection 19(2) or 106(1)
  indicates that the corporation, if it came into existence, would not
  be in compliance with this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 8
- 2001, c. 14, s. 4
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of certificate

**[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** A corporation comes into
existence on the date shown in the certificate of incorporation.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1974-75-76, c. 33, s. 9
- 1978-79, c. 9, s. 1(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Name of corporation

- **[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  word or expression "Limited", "Limitée", "Incorporated", "Incorporée",
  "Corporation" or "Société par actions de régime fédéral" or the
  corresponding abbreviation "Ltd.", "Ltée", "Inc.", "Corp." or
  "S.A.R.F." shall be part, other than only in a figurative or
  descriptive sense, of the name of every corporation, but a corporation
  may use and be legally designated by either the full or the
  corresponding abbreviated form.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Saving for "S.C.C."

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a corporation that
  has a corporate name that, immediately before the day on which this
  subsection comes into force, included, other than only in a figurative
  or descriptive sense, the expression "Société commerciale canadienne"
  or the abbreviation "S.C.C.", and any such corporation may use and be
  legally designated by either that expression or that abbreviation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Director may exempt a body corporate continued as
  a corporation under this Act from the provisions of subsection (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternate name

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection 12(1), the name of a
  corporation may be set out in its articles in an English form, a
  French form, an English form and a French form, or a combined English
  and French form, so long as the combined form meets the prescribed
  criteria. The corporation may use and may be legally designated by any
  such form.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternative name outside Canada

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection 12(1), a corporation may, for
  use outside Canada, set out its name in its articles in any language
  form and it may use and may be legally designated by any such form
  outside Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication of name

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A corporation shall set out its name in legible
  characters in all contracts, invoices, negotiable instruments and
  orders for goods or services issued or made by or on behalf of the
  corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other name

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (5) and 12(1), a corporation
  may carry on business under or identify itself by a name other than
  its corporate name if that other name does not contain, other than in
  a figurative or descriptive sense, either the word or expression
  "Limited", "Limitée", "Incorporated", "Incorporée", "Corporation" or
  "Société par actions de régime fédéral" or the corresponding
  abbreviation.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 10
- 1992, c. 1, s. 53
- 1994, c. 24, s. 5
- 2001, c. 14, s. 5
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reserving name

- **[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Director may, on request, reserve for a prescribed period a name for
  an intended corporation or for a corporation that intends to change
  its name.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Designating number

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If requested to do so by the incorporators or a
  corporation, the Director shall assign to the corporation as its name
  a designating number followed by the word "Canada" and a word or
  expression, or the corresponding abbreviation, referred to in
  subsection 10(1).

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 11
- 1994, c. 24, s. 6
- [2018, c. 8, s. 3](#1037619-1373793){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037619-1373793.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-11-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 11"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prohibited names

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  corporation shall not be incorporated or continued as a corporation
  under this Act with, change its name to, or have, carry on business
  under or identify itself by a name that is prohibited by the
  regulations or that does not meet the prescribed requirements.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Directing change of name

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Director may direct a corporation to change its
  name in accordance with section 173 if, through inadvertence or
  otherwise, the corporation acquires a name that is prohibited by the
  regulations or that does not meet the prescribed requirements.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 1994, c. 24, s. 7\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a corporation has a designating number as its
  name, the Director may direct the corporation to change its name to a
  name other than a designating number in accordance with section 173.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Undertaking to change name

  [(4.1)]{.lawlabel} Where a corporation acquires a name as a result of
  a person undertaking to dissolve or to change names, and the
  undertaking is not honoured, the Director may direct the corporation
  to change its name in accordance with section 173, unless the
  undertaking is honoured within the period specified in subsection (5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revoking name

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a corporation has not followed a directive under
  subsection (2), (4) or (4.1) within the prescribed period, the
  Director may revoke the name of the corporation and assign a name to
  it and, until changed in accordance with section 173, the name of the
  corporation is the name assigned by the Director.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 12
- 1994, c. 24, s. 7
- [2018, c. 8, s. 4](#1037630-1373799){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037630-1373799.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-12-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 12"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of amendment

- **[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When a
  corporation has had its name revoked and a name assigned to it under
  subsection 12(5), the Director shall issue a certificate of amendment
  showing the new name of the corporation and shall give notice of the
  change of name as soon as practicable in a publication generally
  available to the public.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of certificate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The articles of the corporation are amended
  accordingly on the date shown in the certificate of amendment.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 13
- 2001, c. 14, s. 6
- 2018, c. 8, s. 5(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-13-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 13"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Personal liability

- **[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this section, a person
  who enters into, or purports to enter into, a written contract in the
  name of or on behalf of a corporation before it comes into existence
  is personally bound by the contract and is entitled to its benefits.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pre-incorporation and pre-amalgamation
  contracts

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A corporation may, within a reasonable time after it
  comes into existence, by any action or conduct signifying its
  intention to be bound thereby, adopt a written contract made before it
  came into existence in its name or on its behalf, and on such adoption

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the corporation is bound by the contract and is
    entitled to the benefits thereof as if the corporation had been in
    existence at the date of the contract and had been a party thereto;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person who purported to act in the name of or on
    behalf of the corporation ceases, except as provided in subsection
    (3), to be bound by or entitled to the benefits of the contract.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to court

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (4), whether or not a written
  contract made before the coming into existence of a corporation is
  adopted by the corporation, a party to the contract may apply to a
  court for an order respecting the nature and extent of the obligations
  and liability under the contract of the corporation and the person who
  entered into, or purported to enter into, the contract in the name of
  or on behalf of the corporation. On the application, the court may
  make any order it thinks fit.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption from personal liability

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If expressly so provided in the written contract, a
  person who purported to act in the name of or on behalf of the
  corporation before it came into existence is not in any event bound by
  the contract or entitled to the benefits thereof.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 14
- 2001, c. 14, s. 7
:::

## [PART III]{.HLabel1}[Capacity and Powers]{.HTitleText1} {#h-108591 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Capacity of a corporation

- **[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  corporation has the capacity and, subject to this Act, the rights,
  powers and privileges of a natural person.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A corporation may carry on business throughout
  Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extra-territorial capacity

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A corporation has the capacity to carry on its
  business, conduct its affairs and exercise its powers in any
  jurisdiction outside Canada to the extent that the laws of such
  jurisdiction permit.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 15
- 2011, c. 21, s. 14(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-15-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 15"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of a corporation

- **[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} It is
  not necessary for a by-law to be passed in order to confer any
  particular power on the corporation or its directors.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restricted business or powers

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A corporation shall not carry on any business or
  exercise any power that it is restricted by its articles from carrying
  on or exercising, nor shall the corporation exercise any of its powers
  in a manner contrary to its articles.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rights preserved

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No act of a corporation, including any transfer of
  property to or by a corporation, is invalid by reason only that the
  act or transfer is contrary to its articles or this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1974-75-76, c. 33, s. 16
- 1978-79, c. 9, s. 1(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No constructive notice

**[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** No person is affected by
or is deemed to have notice or knowledge of the contents of a document
concerning a corporation by reason only that the document has been filed
by the Director or is available for inspection at an office of the
corporation.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1974-75-76, c. 33, s. 17
- 1978-79, c. 9, s. 1(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authority of directors, officers and
agents

- **[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} No
  corporation and no guarantor of an obligation of a corporation may
  assert against a person dealing with the corporation or against a
  person who acquired rights from the corporation that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the articles, by-laws and any unanimous shareholder
    agreement have not been complied with;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the persons named in the most recent notice sent to
    the Director under section 106 or 113 are not the directors of the
    corporation;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the place named in the most recent notice sent to
    the Director under section 19 is not the registered office of the
    corporation;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a person held out by a corporation as a director,
    officer, agent or mandatary of the corporation has not been duly
    appointed or has no authority to exercise the powers and perform the
    duties that are customary in the business of the corporation or
    usual for a director, officer, agent or mandatary;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a document issued by any director, officer, agent
    or mandatary of a corporation with actual or usual authority to
    issue the document is not valid or genuine; or

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a sale, lease or exchange of property referred to
    in subsection 189(3) was not authorized.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of a person
  who has, or ought to have, knowledge of a situation described in that
  subsection by virtue of their relationship to the corporation.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 18
- 2001, c. 14, s. 8
- 2011, c. 21, s. 15(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-18-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 18"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART IV]{.HLabel1}[Registered Office and Records]{.HTitleText1} {#h-108627 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registered office

- **[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  corporation shall at all times have a registered office in the
  province in Canada specified in its articles.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of registered office

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A notice of registered office in the form that the
  Director fixes shall be sent to the Director together with any
  articles that designate or change the province where the registered
  office of the corporation is located.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Change of address

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The directors of a corporation may change the place
  and address of the registered office within the province specified in
  the articles.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of change of address

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A corporation shall send to the Director, within
  fifteen days of any change of address of its registered office, a
  notice in the form that the Director fixes and the Director shall file
  it.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 19
- 2001, c. 14, s. 9
- 2018, c. 8, s. 6(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-19-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 19"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Corporate records

- **[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  corporation shall prepare and maintain, at its registered office or at
  any other place in Canada designated by the directors, records
  containing

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the articles and the by-laws, and all amendments
    thereto, and a copy of any unanimous shareholder agreement;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} minutes of meetings and resolutions of
    shareholders;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} copies of all notices required by section 106 or
    113; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a securities register that complies with section
    50.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Directors records

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In addition to the records described in subsection
  (1), a corporation shall prepare and maintain adequate accounting
  records and records containing minutes of meetings and resolutions of
  the directors and any committee thereof.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Retention of accounting records

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to any other Act of Parliament and to any
  Act of the legislature of a province that provides for a longer
  retention period, a corporation shall retain the accounting records
  referred to in subsection (2) for a period of six years after the end
  of the financial year to which the records relate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Records of continued corporations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of paragraph (1)(b) and subsection
  (2), where a body corporate is continued under this Act, "records"
  includes similar records required by law to be maintained by the body
  corporate before it was so continued.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Place of directors records

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The records described in subsection (2) shall be kept
  at the registered office of the corporation or at such other place as
  the directors think fit and shall at all reasonable times be open to
  inspection by the directors.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Records in Canada

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If accounting records of a corporation are kept
  outside Canada, accounting records adequate to enable the directors to
  ascertain the financial position of the corporation with reasonable
  accuracy on a quarterly basis shall be kept at the registered office
  or any other place in Canada designated by the directors.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When records or registers kept outside
  Canada

  [(5.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsections (1) and (5), but subject to the
  [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3), the [Excise Tax
  Act](/eng/acts/E-15), the [Customs Act](/eng/acts/C-52.6) and any
  other Act administered by the Minister of National Revenue, a
  corporation may keep all or any of its corporate records and
  accounting records referred to in subsection (1) or (2) at a place
  outside Canada, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the records are available for inspection, by means
    of a computer terminal or other technology, during regular office
    hours at the registered office or any other place in Canada
    designated by the directors; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the corporation provides the technical assistance
    to facilitate an inspection referred to in paragraph (a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offence

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A corporation that, without reasonable cause, fails
  to comply with this section is guilty of an offence and liable on
  summary conviction to a fine not exceeding five thousand dollars.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 20
- 1994, c. 24, s. 8
- 2001, c. 14, s. 10
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Access to corporate records

- **[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (1.1),
  shareholders and creditors of a corporation, their personal
  representatives and the Director may examine the records described in
  subsection 20(1) during the usual business hours of the corporation,
  and may take extracts from the records, free of charge, and, if the
  corporation is a distributing corporation, any other person may do so
  on payment of a reasonable fee.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement for affidavit ---
  securities register

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Any person described in subsection (1) who wishes
  to examine the securities register of a distributing corporation must
  first make a request to the corporation or its agent or mandatary,
  accompanied by an affidavit referred to in subsection (7). On receipt
  of the affidavit, the corporation or its agent or mandatary shall
  allow the applicant access to the securities register during the
  corporation's usual business hours, and, on payment of a reasonable
  fee, provide the applicant with an extract from the securities
  register.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies of corporate records

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A shareholder of a corporation is entitled on request
  and without charge to one copy of the articles and by-laws and of any
  unanimous shareholder agreement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shareholder lists

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Shareholders and creditors of a corporation, their
  personal representatives, the Director and, if the corporation is a
  distributing corporation, any other person, on payment of a reasonable
  fee and on sending to a corporation or its agent or mandatary the
  affidavit referred to in subsection (7), may on application require
  the corporation or its agent or mandatary to provide within 10 days
  after the receipt of the affidavit a list (in this section referred to
  as the "basic list") made up to a date not more than 10 days before
  the date of receipt of the affidavit setting out the names of the
  shareholders of the corporation, the number of shares owned by each
  shareholder and the address of each shareholder as shown on the
  records of the corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Supplemental lists

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A person requiring a corporation to provide a basic
  list may, by stating in the affidavit referred to in subsection (3)
  that they require supplemental lists, require the corporation or its
  agent or mandatary on payment of a reasonable fee to provide
  supplemental lists setting out any changes from the basic list in the
  names or addresses of the shareholders and the number of shares owned
  by each shareholder for each business day following the date the basic
  list is made up to.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}When supplemental lists to be provided

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The corporation or its agent or mandatary shall
  provide a supplemental list required under subsection (4)

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on the date the basic list is furnished, where the
    information relates to changes that took place prior to that date;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} on the business day following the day to which the
    supplemental list relates, where the information relates to changes
    that take place on or after the date the basic list is furnished.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Holders of options

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A person requiring a corporation to furnish a basic
  list or a supplemental list may also require the corporation to
  include in that list the name and address of any known holder of an
  option or right to acquire shares of the corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contents of affidavit

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} The affidavit required under subsection (1.1) or (3)
  shall state

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the applicant;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name and address for service of the body
    corporate, if the applicant is a body corporate; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that the basic list and any supplemental lists
    obtained pursuant to subsection (4) or the information contained in
    the securities register obtained pursuant to subsection (1.1), as
    the case may be, will not be used except as permitted under
    subsection (9).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} If the applicant is a body corporate, the affidavit
  shall be made by a director or officer of the body corporate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use of information or shareholder list

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} A list of shareholders or information from a
  securities register obtained under this section shall not be used by
  any person except in connection with

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an effort to influence the voting of shareholders
    of the corporation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an offer to acquire securities of the corporation;
    or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any other matter relating to the affairs of the
    corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offence

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} A person who, without reasonable cause, contravenes
  this section is guilty of an offence and liable on summary conviction
  to a fine not exceeding five thousand dollars or to imprisonment for a
  term not exceeding six months or to both.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 21
- 2001, c. 14, ss. 11, 135(E)
- 2011, c. 21, s. 16(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Register

- **[[21.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The corporation shall prepare and
  maintain, at its registered office or at any other place in Canada
  designated by the directors, a register of individuals with
  significant control over the corporation that contains

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for each individual with significant control,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} their name and date of birth,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} their residential address, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} their address for service, if it has been
      provided to the corporation;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} the citizenship of each individual with
    significant control;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the jurisdiction of residence for tax purposes of
    each individual with significant control;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the day on which each individual became or ceased
    to be an individual with significant control, as the case may be;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a description of how each individual is an
    individual with significant control over the corporation, including,
    as applicable, a description of their interests and rights in
    respect of shares of the corporation;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any other prescribed information; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a description of each step taken in accordance with
    subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Updating of information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The corporation shall, at the following times, take
  reasonable steps to ensure that it has identified all individuals with
  significant control over the corporation and that the information in
  the register is accurate, complete and up-to-date:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} at least once during each financial year of the
    corporation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} on the request of the Director; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} at the times provided for in the regulations.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Recording of information

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the corporation becomes aware of any information
  referred to in paragraphs (1)(a) to (e) as a result of steps taken in
  accordance with subsection (2) or through any other means, the
  corporation shall record that information in the register within 15
  days of becoming aware of it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information from shareholders

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the corporation requests information referred to
  in any of paragraphs (1)(a) to (e) from one of its shareholders, the
  shareholder shall, to the best of their knowledge, reply accurately
  and completely as soon as feasible.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disposal of personal information

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Within one year after the sixth anniversary of the
  day on which an individual ceases to be an individual with significant
  control over the corporation, the corporation shall --- subject to any
  other Act of Parliament and to any Act of the legislature of a
  province that provides for a longer retention period --- dispose of
  any of that individual's [[personal
  information]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in subsection 2(1) of
  the [Personal Information Protection and Electronic Documents
  Act](/eng/acts/P-8.6), that is recorded in the register.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offence

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A corporation that, without reasonable cause,
  contravenes this section is guilty of an offence and liable on summary
  conviction to a fine not exceeding \$100,000.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} This section does not apply to a corporation

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that is a reporting issuer or an *[émetteur
    assujetti]{.otherLang lang="fr"}* under an Act of the legislature of
    a province relating to the regulation of securities;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} any of the securities of which are listed and
    posted for trading on a [[designated stock
    exchange]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in subsection 248(1) of
    the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that is a member of a prescribed class.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inability to identify individuals

**[[21.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21.2 .sectionLabel}** A corporation to
which section 21.1 applies shall take prescribed steps if it is unable
to identify any individuals with significant control over the
corporation.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 27, s. 183](#1039120-1144079){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039120-1144079.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sending of information to Director

- **[[21.21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21.21
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A corporation to which section 21.1
  applies shall send to the Director

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on an annual basis, the information determined by
    the Director from among that in its register of individuals with
    significant control over the corporation, in the form and within the
    period that the Director fixes; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the information determined by the Director from
    among that recorded under subsection 21.1(3), within 15 days after
    the day on which it is recorded and in the form that the Director
    fixes.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sending of information --- certificates
  issued

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} On or after the date shown on a certificate referred
  to in section 8 or subsection 185(4) or 187(4), a corporation to which
  section 21.1 applies shall send to the Director the information
  determined by the Director from among that referred to in paragraphs
  21.1(1)(a) to (f), in the form and within the period that the Director
  fixes.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Period for keeping and producing
  information

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Director is not required to keep or produce any
  information received under subsection (1) or (2) after the end of the
  six-year period following the day on which it is received.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offence

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A corporation that, without reasonable cause,
  contravenes subsection (1) or (2) is guilty of an offence and liable
  on summary conviction to a fine not exceeding \$100,000.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 431](#1358026-1438905){.wb-lbx

  role="button"}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure to Director

- **[[21.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21.3 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  corporation to which section 21.1 applies shall disclose to the
  Director, on request, any information in its register of individuals
  with significant control.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2023, c. 29, s. 3\]]{.Repealed}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provision of information by Director

**[[21.301]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21.301 .sectionLabel}** The Director may
provide all or part of the information received under section 21.21 to
an investigative body referred to in subsection 21.31(2), the Financial
Transactions and Reports Analysis Centre of Canada or any prescribed
entity.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2022, c. 10, s. 432](#1358028-1439010){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1358028-1439010.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provision of information by Director

**[[21.302]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21.302 .sectionLabel}** The Director may
provide all or part of the information received under section 21.21 to a
provincial corporate registry or a provincial government department or
agency that is responsible for corporate law in that province.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 29, s. 4](#1426303-1438928){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1426303-1438928.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information available to public

- **[[21.303]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21.303
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Director shall make available to
  the public the following information sent to the Director under
  section 21.21 for each individual with significant control:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} their name;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} their address for service, if it has been provided
    to the corporation;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} their residential address, if their address for
    service has not been provided to the corporation;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the information referred to in paragraphs
    21.1(1)(c) and (d); and

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} any other prescribed information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of an
  individual who is less than 18 years of age or an individual to whom
  prescribed circumstances apply.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption on application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If an individual with significant control or the
  corporation over which they have significant control applies to the
  Director to have any information referred to in subsection (1) in
  relation to the individual not made available to the public, the
  Director may choose to not make it available to the public, subject to
  any terms that the Director thinks fit,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the Director reasonably believes that making it
    available presents or would present a serious threat to the safety
    of the individual; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the Director is satisfied that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the individual is incapable,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the information is to be kept confidential under
      subsection 27(8) of the [Conflict of Interest
      Act](/eng/acts/C-36.65) or a similar provision of an Act of the
      legislature of a province, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} prescribed circumstances apply to the
      individual.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 29, s. 4](#1426303-1438947){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1426303-1438947.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Disclosure to investigative bodies

- **[[21.31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21.31
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} On request by an investigative body
  referred to in subsection (2), a corporation to which section 21.1
  applies shall, as soon as feasible after the request is served on the
  corporation or deemed to be received by it, and in the manner
  specified by the investigative body,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} provide the investigative body with a copy of the
    corporation's register of individuals with significant control; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} disclose to the investigative body any information
    specified by the investigative body that is in the corporation's
    register of individuals with significant control.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Investigative bodies

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The investigative bodies for the purpose of this
  section are

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any police force;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Canada Revenue Agency and any provincial body
    that has responsibilities similar to those of the Canada Revenue
    Agency; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any prescribed body that has investigative powers
    in relation to offences referred to in the schedule.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An investigative body may make a request only if it
  has reasonable grounds to suspect that the copy of the register or the
  specified information would be relevant to investigating an offence
  referred to in the schedule and it also has reasonable grounds to
  suspect that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the corporation that is the subject of the request
    committed the offence or was used to

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} commit the offence,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} facilitate the commission of the offence, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} protect from detection or punishment a person
      who has committed the offence;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an individual with significant control over the
    corporation that is the subject of the request is also an individual
    with significant control over a corporation that committed the
    offence or was used to do anything referred to in any of
    subparagraphs (a)(i) to (iii); or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an individual with significant control over the
    corporation that is the subject of the request is also an individual
    who, directly or indirectly, influences the affairs of an entity,
    other than a corporation, that committed the offence or was used to
    do anything referred to in any of subparagraphs (a)(i) to (iii).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Service or sending of request

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The request must be served on the corporation by
  leaving the request at the corporation's registered office as shown in
  the last notice filed under section 19 or sent to the corporation by
  registered mail to that registered office and, if so sent, is deemed
  to be received at the time it would be delivered in the ordinary
  course of mail, unless there are reasonable grounds for believing that
  the corporation did not receive the request at that time or at all.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offence

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A corporation that, without reasonable cause,
  contravenes subsection (1) is guilty of an offence and liable on
  summary conviction to a fine of not more than \$100,000.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendment to schedule

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, by order, amend the
  schedule by adding or deleting a reference to an offence.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 29, s. 99](#1155665-1173143){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1155665-1173143.html"
  role="button"}
- [2024, c. 17, s. 233](#1467789-1479902){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1467789-1479902.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-21.31-20190621.html "Link to previous version of section 21.31"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Record

- **[[21.32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21.32
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every investigative body that makes
  a request under subsection 21.31(1) shall keep a record setting out
  the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name of the corporation that was the subject of
    the request;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the reasonable grounds on which the request was
    based;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} information respecting what was requested;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the date the request was served or deemed to have
    been received;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} information respecting the service or the sending
    of the request;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} all information received from the corporation in
    response to the request; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} any prescribed information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Report

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every investigative body that makes a request under
  subsection 21.31(1) shall, within 90 days after the end the calendar
  year in which the request was made, provide the Director with a report
  setting out the total number of requests made by it in that year and,
  in the case of the Royal Canadian Mounted Police and the Canada
  Revenue Agency, the number of requests made in each province.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Section 251 does not apply in respect of a
  contravention of subsection (1) or (2).

::: HistoricalNote
- [2019, c. 29, s. 99](#1155665-1173159){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1155665-1173159.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offence

- **[[21.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every director or officer of a
  corporation who knowingly authorizes, permits or acquiesces in the
  contravention of subsection 21.1(1), 21.21(1) or (2), 21.3(1) or
  21.31(1) by that corporation commits an offence, whether or not the
  corporation has been prosecuted or convicted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offence --- recording of false or
  misleading information

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every director or officer of a corporation who
  knowingly records or knowingly authorizes, permits or acquiesces in
  the recording of false or misleading information in the register of
  the corporation referred to in subsection 21.1(1) commits an offence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offence --- provision of false or
  misleading information

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Every director or officer of a corporation who
  knowingly provides or knowingly authorizes, permits or acquiesces in
  the provision to any person or entity of false or misleading
  information in relation to the register of the corporation referred to
  in subsection 21.1(1) commits an offence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offence --- subsection 21.1(4)

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Every shareholder who knowingly contravenes
  subsection 21.1(4) commits an offence.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offence

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A person who commits an offence under any of
  subsections (1) to (4) is liable

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} on summary conviction to a fine not exceeding
    \$200,000 or to imprisonment for a term not exceeding two years, or
    to both; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} on conviction on indictment, to a fine not
    exceeding \$1,000,000 or to imprisonment for a term not exceeding
    five years, or to both.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2018, c. 27, s. 183](#1039120-1144113){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039120-1144113.html"
  role="button"}
- [2019, c. 29, s. 100](#1155667-1173116){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1155667-1173116.html"
  role="button"}
- [2023, c. 29, s. 5](#1426305-1438948){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1426305-1438948.html"
  role="button"}
- [2024, c. 17, s. 234](#1467791-1479907){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1467791-1479907.html"
  role="button"}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form of records

- **[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} All
  registers and other records required by this Act to be prepared and
  maintained may be in a bound or loose-leaf form or in a photographic
  film form, or may be entered or recorded by any system of mechanical
  or electronic data processing or any other information storage device
  that is capable of reproducing any required information in
  intelligible written form within a reasonable time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Precautions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A corporation or its agents or mandataries shall take
  reasonable precautions to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} prevent loss or destruction of,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} prevent falsification of entries in, and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} facilitate detection and correction of inaccuracies
    in

  the registers and other records required by this Act to be prepared
  and maintained.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offence

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A person who, without reasonable cause, contravenes
  this section is guilty of an offence and liable on summary conviction
  to a fine not exceeding five thousand dollars or to imprisonment for a
  term not exceeding six months or to both.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 22
- 2011, c. 21, s. 17(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-22-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 22"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Corporate seal

- **[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  corporation may, but need not, adopt a corporate seal, and may change
  a corporate seal that is adopted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Validity of unsealed documents

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A document executed or, in Quebec, signed on behalf
  of a corporation is not invalid merely because a corporate seal is not
  affixed to it.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 23
- 2001, c. 14, s. 12
- 2011, c. 21, s. 18(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-23-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 23"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART V]{.HLabel1}[Corporate Finance]{.HTitleText1} {#h-108717 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shares

- **[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Shares
  of a corporation shall be in registered form and shall be without
  nominal or par value.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When a body corporate is continued under this Act, a
  share with nominal or par value issued by the body corporate before it
  was so continued is, for the purpose of subsection (1), deemed to be a
  share without nominal or par value.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rights attached to shares

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Where a corporation has only one class of shares, the
  rights of the holders thereof are equal in all respects and include
  the rights

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to vote at any meeting of shareholders of the
    corporation;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to receive any dividend declared by the
    corporation; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} to receive the remaining property of the
    corporation on dissolution.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Rights to classes of shares

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The articles may provide for more than one class of
  shares and, if they so provide,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the rights, privileges, restrictions and conditions
    attaching to the shares of each class shall be set out therein; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the rights set out in subsection (3) shall be
    attached to at least one class of shares but all such rights are not
    required to be attached to one class.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 24
- R.S., 1985, c. 1 (4th Supp.), s. 45(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Issue of shares

- **[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to the articles, the by-laws
  and any unanimous shareholder agreement and to section 28, shares may
  be issued at such times and to such persons and for such consideration
  as the directors may determine.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Shares non-assessable

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Shares issued by a corporation are non-assessable and
  the holders are not liable to the corporation or to its creditors in
  respect thereof.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consideration

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A share shall not be issued until the consideration
  for the share is fully paid in money or in property or past services
  that are not less in value than the fair equivalent of the money that
  the corporation would have received if the share had been issued for
  money.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consideration other than money

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In determining whether property or past services are
  the fair equivalent of a money consideration, the directors may take
  into account reasonable charges and expenses of organization and
  reorganization and payments for property and past services reasonably
  expected to benefit the corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
  [[property]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section,
  [[property]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} does not include a promissory note,
  or a promise to pay, that is made by a person to whom a share is
  issued, or a person who does not deal at arm's length, within the
  meaning of that expression in the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3),
  with a person to whom a share is issued.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 25
- 2001, c. 14, s. 13
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Stated capital account

- **[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  corporation shall maintain a separate stated capital account for each
  class and series of shares it issues.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Entries in stated capital account

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A corporation shall add to the appropriate stated
  capital account the full amount of any consideration it receives for
  any shares it issues.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception for non-arm's length
  transactions

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (2), a corporation may, subject to
  subsection (4), add to the stated capital accounts maintained for the
  shares of classes or series the whole or any part of the amount of the
  consideration that it receives in an exchange if the corporation
  issues shares

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in exchange for

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} property of a person who immediately before the
      exchange did not deal with the corporation at arm's length within
      the meaning of that expression in the [Income Tax
      Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3),

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} shares of, or another interest or right in, a
      body corporate that immediately before the exchange, or that
      because of the exchange, did not deal with the corporation at
      arm's length within the meaning of that expression in the [Income
      Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3), or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} property of a person who, immediately before
      the exchange, dealt with the corporation at arm's length within
      the meaning of that expression in the [Income Tax
      Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3), if the person, the corporation and all the
      holders of shares in the class or series of shares so issued
      consent to the exchange; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} pursuant to an agreement referred to in subsection
    182(1) or an arrangement referred to in paragraph 192(1)(b) or (c)
    or to shareholders of an amalgamating body corporate who receive the
    shares in addition to or instead of securities of the amalgamated
    body corporate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limit on addition to a stated capital
  account

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} On the issue of a share a corporation shall not add
  to a stated capital account in respect of the share it issues an
  amount greater than the amount of the consideration it received for
  the share.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Constraint on addition to a stated
  capital account

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where a corporation proposes to add any amount to a
  stated capital account it maintains in respect of a class or series of
  shares, if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount to be added was not received by the
    corporation as consideration for the issue of shares, and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the corporation has issued any outstanding shares
    of more than one class or series,

  the addition to the stated capital account must be approved by special
  resolution unless all the issued and outstanding shares are shares of
  not more than two classes of convertible shares referred to in
  subsection 39(5).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other additions to stated capital

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} When a body corporate is continued under this Act, it
  may add to a stated capital account any consideration received by it
  for a share it issued and a corporation at any time may, subject to
  subsection (5), add to a stated capital account any amount it credited
  to a retained earnings or other surplus account.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} When a body corporate is continued under this Act,
  subsection (2) does not apply to the consideration received by it
  before it was so continued unless the share in respect of which the
  consideration is received is issued after the corporation is so
  continued.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} When a body corporate is continued under this Act,
  any amount unpaid in respect of a share issued by the body corporate
  before it was so continued and paid after it was so continued shall be
  added to the stated capital account maintained for the shares of that
  class or series.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Transitional

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 34(2), sections 38 and
  42, and paragraph 185(2)(a), when a body corporate is continued under
  this Act its stated capital is deemed to include the amount that would
  have been included in stated capital if the body corporate had been
  incorporated under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restriction

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} A corporation shall not reduce its stated capital or
  any stated capital account except in the manner provided in this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception for an open-end mutual fund

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) to (10) and any other provisions of
  this Act relating to stated capital do not apply to an open-end mutual
  fund.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[open-end mutual
  fund]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(12)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, [[open-end mutual
  fund]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a distributing corporation that
