[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hearing [in camera]{.otherLang lang="la"}

- **[[232]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-232 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  interested person may apply to the court for an order that a hearing
  conducted by an inspector under this Part be heard [in
  camera]{.otherLang lang="la"} and for directions on any matter arising
  in the investigation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Right to counsel

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person whose conduct is being investigated or who
  is being examined at a hearing conducted by an inspector under this
  Part has a right to be represented by counsel.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1974-75-76, c. 33, s. 225
- 1978-79, c. 9, s. 1(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Criminating statements

**[[233]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-233 .sectionLabel}** No person is excused
from attending and giving evidence and producing documents and records
to an inspector under this Part by reason only that the evidence tends
to criminate that person or subject that person to any proceeding or
penalty, but no such evidence shall be used or is receivable against
that person in any proceeding thereafter instituted against that person
under an Act of Parliament, other than a prosecution under section 132
of the [Criminal Code](/eng/acts/C-46) for perjury in giving the
evidence or a prosecution under section 136 of the [Criminal
Code](/eng/acts/C-46) in respect of the evidence.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 233
- R.S., 1985, c. 27 (1st Supp.), s. 187
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Absolute privilege (defamation)

**[[234]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-234 .sectionLabel}** Any oral or written
statement or report made by an inspector or any other person in an
investigation under this Part has absolute privilege.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1974-75-76, c. 33, s. 227
- 1978-79, c. 9, s. 1(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information respecting ownership and
control

- **[[235]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-235 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the Director is satisfied that, for the purposes of Part XI, XIII or
  XVII, or for the purposes of enforcing any regulation made under
  section 174, there is reason to inquire into the ownership or control
  of a security of a corporation or any of its affiliates, the Director
  may require any person that the Director reasonably believes has or
  has had an interest or right in the security or acts or has acted on
  behalf of a person with such an interest or right to report to him or
  her or to any person the Director designates

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} information that the person has or can reasonably
    be expected to obtain as to present and past interests or rights in
    the security; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the names and addresses of the persons with such an
    interest or right and of any person who acts or has acted in
    relation to the security on their behalf.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Presumption

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), a person is
  deemed to have an interest or right in a security if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person has a right to vote or to acquire or
    dispose of the security or any interest or right in it;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the person's consent is necessary for the exercise
    of the rights or privileges of any other person with an interest or
    right in the security; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any other person with an interest or right in the
    security can be required or is accustomed to exercise rights or
    privileges attached to the security in accordance with the person's
    instructions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Director shall publish in a publication generally
  available to the public the particulars of information obtained by the
  Director under this section, if the particulars

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} are required to be disclosed by this Act or the
    regulations; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} have not previously been so disclosed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offence

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A person who fails to comply with this section is
  guilty of an offence and liable on summary conviction to a fine not
  exceeding five thousand dollars or to imprisonment for a term not
  exceeding six months or to both.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Officers, etc., of bodies corporate

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Where a body corporate commits an offence under
  subsection (4), any director or officer of the body corporate who
  knowingly authorized, permitted or acquiesced in the commission of the
  offence is a party to and guilty of the offence and is liable on
  summary conviction to a fine not exceeding five thousand dollars or to
  imprisonment for a term not exceeding six months or to both, whether
  or not the body corporate has been prosecuted or convicted.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 235
- 2001, c. 14, ss. 114, 135(E)
- 2011, c. 21, s. 66
- 2018, c. 8, s. 33(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-235-20111129.html "Link to previous version of section 235"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Solicitor-client privilege or
professional secrecy

**[[236]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-236 .sectionLabel}** Nothing in this Part
shall be construed as affecting solicitor-client privilege or, in
Quebec, the professional secrecy of advocates and notaries.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 236
- 2001, c. 14, s. 135(E)
- 2011, c. 21, s. 67
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-236-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 236"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inquiries

- **[[237]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-237 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Director may make inquiries of any person relating to compliance with
  this Act and may, as part of any inquiry, require the person to
  provide any records or other documents or information.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Response

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person shall respond to any inquiry made under
  subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 237
- [2023, c. 29, s. 7](#1426321-1438957){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1426321-1438957.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-237-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 237"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART XIX.1]{.HLabel1}[Apportioning Award of Damages]{.HTitleText1} {#h-111718 .Part}

### [Interpretation and Application]{.HTitleText2} {#h-111719 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[237.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-237.1 .sectionLabel}** The definitions in
this section apply in this Part.

[[financial interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[financial interest]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, with respect to a
    corporation, includes

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a security;

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a title to or an interest in capital, assets,
      property, profits, earnings or royalties;

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an option or other interest in, or a subscription
      to, a security;

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an agreement under which the interest of the
      purchaser is valued for purposes of conversion or surrender by
      reference to the value of a proportionate interest in a specified
      portfolio of assets;

    - [(e)]{.lawlabel} an agreement providing that money received will
      be repaid or treated as a subscription for shares, units or
      interests at the option of any person or the corporation;

    - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a profit-sharing agreement or certificate;

    - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a lease, claim or royalty in oil, natural gas or
      mining, or an interest in the lease, claim or royalty;

    - [(h)]{.lawlabel} an income or annuity contract that is not issued
      by an insurance company governed by an Act of Parliament or a law
      of a province;

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an investment contract; and

    - [(j)]{.lawlabel} anything that is prescribed to be a financial
      interest. ([intérêt financier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[financial loss]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[financial loss]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a financial loss
    arising out of an error, omission or misstatement in financial
    information concerning a corporation that is required under this Act
    or the regulations. ([perte financière]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[third party]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[third party]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes any subsequent party
    that is joined in proceedings before a court. ([Version anglaise
    seulement]{.DefinitionOtherLangOnly})

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 115
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application of Part

- **[[237.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-237.2
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} This Part applies to the
  apportionment of damages awarded to a plaintiff for financial loss
  after a court has found more than one defendant or third party
  responsible for the financial loss.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application of Part

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} This Part does not apply to an award of damages to
  any of the following plaintiffs:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} Her Majesty in right of Canada or of a province;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an agent of Her Majesty in right of Canada or of a
    province or a federal or provincial Crown corporation or government
    agency, unless a substantial part of its activities involves
    trading, including making investments in, securities or other
    financial instruments;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a charitable organization, private foundation or
    public foundation within the meaning of subsection 149.1(1) of the
    [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3); or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an unsecured creditor in respect of goods or
    services that the creditor provided to a corporation.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 115
:::

### [Apportionment of Damages]{.HTitleText2} {#h-111748 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Degree of responsibility

- **[[237.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-237.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to this section and sections
  237.4 to 237.6, every defendant or third party who has been found
  responsible for a financial loss is liable to the plaintiff only for
  the portion of the damages that corresponds to their degree of
  responsibility for the loss.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Uncollectable amounts

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If any part of the damages awarded against a
  responsible defendant or third party is uncollectable, the court may,
  on the application of the plaintiff, reallocate that amount to the
  other responsible defendants or third parties, if the application is
  made within one year after the date that the judgment was made
  enforceable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reallocation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The amount that may be reallocated to each of the
  other responsible defendants or third parties under subsection (2) is
  calculated by multiplying the uncollectable amount by the percentage
  that corresponds to the degree of responsibility of that defendant or
  third party for the total financial loss.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Maximum amount

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The maximum amount determined under subsection (3),
  in respect of any responsible defendant or third party, may not be
  more than fifty per cent of the amount originally awarded against that
  responsible defendant or third party.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 115
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- fraud

- **[[237.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-237.4
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The plaintiff may recover the whole
  amount of the damages awarded by the court from any defendant or third
  party who has been held responsible for a financial loss if it was
  established that the defendant or third party acted fraudulently or
  dishonestly.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contribution

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The defendant or third party referred to in
  subsection (1) is entitled to claim contribution from any other
  defendant or third party who is held responsible for the loss.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 115
:::

### [Joint and Several, or Solidary, Liability]{.HTitleText2} {#h-111767 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Individual or personal body corporate

- **[[237.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-237.5
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Defendants and third parties
  referred to in subsection 237.2(1) are jointly and severally, or
  solidarily, liable for the damages awarded to a plaintiff who is an
  individual or a personal body corporate and who

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} had a financial interest in a corporation on the
    day that an error, omission or misstatement in financial information
    concerning the corporation occurred, or acquired a financial
    interest in the period between the day that the error, omission or
    misstatement occurred and the day, as determined by the court, that
    it was generally disclosed; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has established that the value of the plaintiff's
    total financial interest in the corporation was not more than the
    prescribed amount at the close of business on the day that the
    error, omission or misstatement occurred or at the close of business
    on any day that the plaintiff acquired a financial interest in the
    period referred to in paragraph (a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply when the plaintiff
  brings the action as a member of a partnership or other association or
  as a trustee in bankruptcy, liquidator, receiver or sequestrator of a
  body corporate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a personal body corporate is a body corporate that
    is not actively engaged in any financial, commercial or industrial
    business and that is controlled by an individual, or by a group of
    individuals who are connected by marriage, common-law partnership or
    any legal parent-child relationship or are connected indirectly by a
    combination of those relationships, whether or not the individuals
    through whom they are connected are members of the group; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a common-law partnership is a relationship between
    two persons who are cohabiting with each other in a conjugal
    relationship and have done so for a period of at least one year.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 115
- 2005, c. 33, s. 5
- 2011, c. 21, s. 68(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-237.5-20050720.html "Link to previous version of section 237.5"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Equitable grounds

- **[[237.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-237.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If the value of the plaintiff's
  total financial interest referred to in subsection 237.5(1) is greater
  than the prescribed amount, a court may nevertheless determine that
  the defendants and third parties are jointly and severally, or
  solidarily, liable if the court considers that it is just and
  reasonable to do so.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Factors

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may establish factors that
  the court shall take into account in deciding whether to hold the
  defendants and third parties jointly and severally, or solidarily,
  liable.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Statutory Instruments
  Act](/eng/acts/S-22)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The [Statutory Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22) does
  not apply to the factors referred to in subsection (2), but the
  factors shall be published in Part I of the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 115
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Value of security

- **[[237.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-237.7
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} When, in order to establish the
  value of the total financial interest referred to in subsection
  237.5(1), it is necessary to determine the value of a security that is
  traded on an organized market, the value of the security is, on the
  day specified in subsection (3),

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the closing price of that class of security;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if no closing price is given, the average of the
    highest and lowest prices of that class of security; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the security was not traded, the average of the
    bid and ask prices of that class of security.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Court may adjust value

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The court may adjust the value of a security that has
  been determined under subsection (1) when the court considers it
  reasonable to do so.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Valuation day

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The value of the security is to be determined as of
  the day that the error, omission or misstatement occurred. If the
  security was acquired in the period between that day and the day, as
  determined by the court, that the error, omission or misstatement was
  generally disclosed, the value is to be determined as of the day that
  it was acquired.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of [[organized
  market]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[organized
  market]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a recognized exchange for a class
  of securities or a market that regularly publishes the price of that
  class of securities in a publication that is generally available to
  the public.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 115
- 2018, c. 8, s. 34(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-237.7-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 237.7"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Court determines value

- **[[237.8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-237.8
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The court shall determine the value
  of all or any part of a financial interest that is subject to resale
  restrictions or for which there is no organized market.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Factors

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may establish factors that
  the court may take into account in determining value under subsection
  (1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}[Statutory Instruments
  Act](/eng/acts/S-22)

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The [Statutory Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22) does
  not apply to the factors referred to in subsection (2), but the
  factors shall be published in Part I of the [Canada
  Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 115
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to determine value

**[[237.9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-237.9 .sectionLabel}** The plaintiff may,
by application made at any time before or during the course of the
proceedings, request the court to determine the value of the plaintiff's
financial interest for the purpose of subsection 237.5(1).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 115
:::

## [PART XX]{.HLabel1}[Remedies, Offences and Punishment]{.HTitleText1} {#h-111817 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[238]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-238 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,

[[action]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[action]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an action under this
    Act; ([action]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[complainant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[complainant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

    - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a registered holder or beneficial owner, and a
      former registered holder or beneficial owner, of a security of a
      corporation or any of its affiliates,

    - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a director or an officer or a former director or
      officer of a corporation or any of its affiliates,

    - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the Director, or

    - [(d)]{.lawlabel} any other person who, in the discretion of a
      court, is a proper person to make an application under this
      Part. ([plaignant]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- 1974-75-76, c. 33, s. 231
- 1978-79, c. 9, s. 1(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Commencing derivative action

- **[[239]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-239
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), a
  complainant may apply to a court for leave to bring an action in the
  name and on behalf of a corporation or any of its subsidiaries, or
  intervene in an action to which any such body corporate is a party,
  for the purpose of prosecuting, defending or discontinuing the action
  on behalf of the body corporate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions precedent

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} No action may be brought and no intervention in an
  action may be made under subsection (1) unless the court is satisfied
  that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the complainant has given notice to the directors
    of the corporation or its subsidiary of the complainant's intention
    to apply to the court under subsection (1) not less than fourteen
    days before bringing the application, or as otherwise ordered by the
    court, if the directors of the corporation or its subsidiary do not
    bring, diligently prosecute or defend or discontinue the action;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the complainant is acting in good faith; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it appears to be in the interests of the
    corporation or its subsidiary that the action be brought,
    prosecuted, defended or discontinued.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 239
- 2001, c. 14, s. 116
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of court

**[[240]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-240 .sectionLabel}** In connection with an
action brought or intervened in under section 239, the court may at any
time make any order it thinks fit including, without limiting the
generality of the foregoing,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an order authorizing the complainant or any other
  person to control the conduct of the action;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} an order giving directions for the conduct of the
  action;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} an order directing that any amount adjudged payable
  by a defendant in the action shall be paid, in whole or in part,
  directly to former and present security holders of the corporation or
  its subsidiary instead of to the corporation or its subsidiary; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} an order requiring the corporation or its subsidiary
  to pay reasonable legal fees incurred by the complainant in connection
  with the action.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1974-75-76, c. 33, s. 233
- 1978-79, c. 9, s. 1(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to court re oppression

- **[[241]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-241 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  complainant may apply to a court for an order under this section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Grounds

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, on an application under subsection (1), the court
  is satisfied that in respect of a corporation or any of its affiliates

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} any act or omission of the corporation or any of
    its affiliates effects a result,

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the business or affairs of the corporation or any
    of its affiliates are or have been carried on or conducted in a
    manner, or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the powers of the directors of the corporation or
    any of its affiliates are or have been exercised in a manner

  that is oppressive or unfairly prejudicial to or that unfairly
  disregards the interests of any security holder, creditor, director or
  officer, the court may make an order to rectify the matters complained
  of.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of court

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In connection with an application under this section,
  the court may make any interim or final order it thinks fit including,
  without limiting the generality of the foregoing,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an order restraining the conduct complained of;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an order appointing a receiver or receiver-manager;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an order to regulate a corporation's affairs by
    amending the articles or by-laws or creating or amending a unanimous
    shareholder agreement;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an order directing an issue or exchange of
    securities;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} an order appointing directors in place of or in
    addition to all or any of the directors then in office;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} an order directing a corporation, subject to
    subsection (6), or any other person, to purchase securities of a
    security holder;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} an order directing a corporation, subject to
    subsection (6), or any other person, to pay a security holder any
    part of the monies that the security holder paid for securities;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} an order varying or setting aside a transaction or
    contract to which a corporation is a party and compensating the
    corporation or any other party to the transaction or contract;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an order requiring a corporation, within a time
    specified by the court, to produce to the court or an interested
    person financial statements in the form required by section 155 or
    an accounting in such other form as the court may determine;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} an order compensating an aggrieved person;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} an order directing rectification of the registers
    or other records of a corporation under section 243;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} an order liquidating and dissolving the
    corporation;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} an order directing an investigation under Part XIX
    to be made; and

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} an order requiring the trial of any issue.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Duty of directors

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If an order made under this section directs amendment
  of the articles or by-laws of a corporation,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the directors shall forthwith comply with
    subsection 191(4); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} no other amendment to the articles or by-laws shall
    be made without the consent of the court, until a court otherwise
    orders.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exclusion

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A shareholder is not entitled to dissent under
  section 190 if an amendment to the articles is effected under this
  section.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} A corporation shall not make a payment to a
  shareholder under paragraph (3)(f) or (g) if there are reasonable
  grounds for believing that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the corporation is or would after that payment be
    unable to pay its liabilities as they become due; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the realizable value of the corporation's assets
    would thereby be less than the aggregate of its liabilities.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alternative order

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} An applicant under this section may apply in the
  alternative for an order under section 214.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 241
- 2001, c. 14, ss. 117(F), 135(E)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Evidence of shareholder approval not
decisive

- **[[242]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-242 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  application made or an action brought or intervened in under this Part
  shall not be stayed or dismissed by reason only that it is shown that
  an alleged breach of a right or duty owed to the corporation or its
  subsidiary has been or may be approved by the shareholders of such
  body corporate, but evidence of approval by the shareholders may be
  taken into account by the court in making an order under section 214,
  240 or 241.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Court approval to discontinue

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An application made or an action brought or
  intervened in under this Part shall not be stayed, discontinued,
  settled or dismissed for want of prosecution or, in Quebec, failure to
  respect the agreement between the parties as to the conduct of the
  proceeding without the approval of the court given on any terms that
  the court thinks fit and, if the court determines that the interests
  of any complainant may be substantially affected by such stay,
  discontinuance, settlement, dismissal or failure, the court may order
  any party to the application or action to give notice to the
  complainant.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No security for costs

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A complainant is not required to give security for
  costs in any application made or action brought or intervened in under
  this Part.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interim costs

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} In an application made or an action brought or
  intervened in under this Part, the court may at any time order the
  corporation or its subsidiary to pay to the complainant interim costs,
  including legal fees and disbursements, but the complainant may be
  held accountable for such interim costs on final disposition of the
  application or action.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 242
- 2001, c. 14, s. 118(F)
- 2011, c. 21, s. 69
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-242-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 242"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to court to rectify records

- **[[243]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-243 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the name of a person is alleged to be or to have been wrongly entered
  or retained in, or wrongly deleted or omitted from, the registers or
  other records of a corporation, the corporation, a security holder of
  the corporation or any aggrieved person may apply to a court for an
  order that the registers or records be rectified.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to Director

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An applicant under this section shall give the
  Director notice of the application and the Director is entitled to
  appear and be heard in person or by counsel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Powers of court

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In connection with an application under this section,
  the court may make any order it thinks fit including, without limiting
  the generality of the foregoing,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an order requiring the registers or other records
    of the corporation to be rectified;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an order restraining the corporation from calling
    or holding a meeting of shareholders or paying a dividend before
    such rectification;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an order determining the right of a party to the
    proceedings to have their name entered or retained in, or deleted or
    omitted from, the registers or records of the corporation, whether
    the issue arises between two or more security holders or alleged
    security holders, or between the corporation and any security
    holders or alleged security holders; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an order compensating a party who has incurred a
    loss.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 243
- 2001, c. 14, s. 135(E)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for directions

**[[244]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-244 .sectionLabel}** The Director may apply
to a court for directions in respect of any matter concerning the
Director's duties under this Act, and on such application the court may
give such directions and make such further order as it thinks fit.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 244
- 2001, c. 14, s. 135(E)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of refusal by Director

- **[[245]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-245 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  the Director refuses to file any articles or other document that this
  Act requires the Director to file before the articles or other
  document become effective, the Director shall, within twenty days
  after receiving them or twenty days after receiving any approval that
  may be required under any other Act, whichever is later, give written
  notice of the refusal to the person who sent the articles or document,
  giving reasons.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed refusal

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Director does not file or give written notice
  of the refusal to file any articles or document within the time
  limited therefor in subsection (1), the Director is deemed for the
  purposes of section 246 to have refused to file the articles or
  document.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 245
- 2001, c. 14, s. 135(E)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal from Director's decision

**[[246]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-246 .sectionLabel}** A person who feels
aggrieved by a decision of the Director referred to in any of paragraphs
(a) to (g) may apply to a court for an order, including an order
requiring the Director to change the decision

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} to refuse to file in the form submitted any articles
  or other document required by this Act to be filed;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} to give a name, to change or revoke a name, or to
  refuse to reserve, accept, change or revoke a name under section 12;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} to grant, or to refuse to grant, an exemption that
  may be granted under this Act and the regulations;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} to refuse under subsection 187(11) to permit a
  continued reference to shares having a nominal or par value;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} to refuse to issue a certificate of discontinuance
  under section 188 or a certificate attesting that as of a certain date
  the corporation exists under subsection 263.1(2);

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} to issue, or to refuse to issue, a certificate of
  revival under section 209, or the decision with respect to the terms
  for revival imposed by the Director;

- [(f.1)]{.lawlabel} to correct, or to refuse to correct, articles, a
  notice, a certificate or other document under section 265;

- [(f.2)]{.lawlabel} to cancel, or to refuse to cancel, the articles and
  related certificate under section 265.1; or

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} to dissolve a corporation under section 212.

The Court may make any order it thinks fit.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 246
- 1999, c. 31, s. 65
- 2001, c. 14, s. 119
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Restraining or compliance order

**[[247]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-247 .sectionLabel}** If a corporation or any
director, officer, employee, agent or mandatary, auditor, trustee,
receiver, receiver-manager, sequestrator or liquidator of a corporation
does not comply with this Act, the regulations, articles or by-laws, or
a unanimous shareholder agreement, a complainant or a creditor of the
corporation may, in addition to any other right they have, apply to a
court for an order directing any such person to comply with, or
restraining any such person from acting in breach of, any provisions of
this Act, the regulations, articles or by-laws, or a unanimous
shareholder agreement, and on such application the court may so order
and make any further order it thinks fit.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 247
- 2001, c. 14, s. 135(E)
- 2011, c. 21, s. 70(E)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-247-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 247"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Summary application to court

**[[248]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-248 .sectionLabel}** Where this Act states
that a person may apply to a court, the application may be made in a
summary manner by petition, originating notice of motion, or otherwise
as the rules of the court provide, and subject to any order respecting
notice to interested parties or costs, or any other order the court
thinks fit.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1974-75-76, c. 33, s. 241
- 1978-79, c. 9, s. 1(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal of final order

- **[[249]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-249 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  appeal lies to the court of appeal of a province from any final order
  made by a court of that province under this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appeal with leave

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} An appeal lies to the court of appeal of a province
  from any order other than a final order made by a court of that
  province, only with leave of the court of appeal in accordance with
  the rules applicable to that court.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 249
- 2001, c. 14, s. 120
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offences with respect to reports

- **[[250]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-250 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who makes or assists in making a report, return, notice or
  other document required by this Act or the regulations to be sent to
  the Director or to any other person that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} contains an untrue statement of a material fact, or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} omits to state a material fact required therein or
    necessary to make a statement contained therein not misleading in
    the light of the circumstances in which it was made

  is guilty of an offence and liable on summary conviction to a fine not
  exceeding five thousand dollars or to imprisonment for a term not
  exceeding six months or to both.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Officers, etc., of bodies corporate

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Where a body corporate commits an offence under
  subsection (1), any director or officer of the body corporate who
  knowingly authorized, permitted or acquiesced in the commission of the
  offence is a party to and guilty of the offence and is liable on
  summary conviction to a fine not exceeding five thousand dollars or to
  imprisonment for a term not exceeding six months or to both, whether
  or not the body corporate has been prosecuted or convicted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Immunity

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No person is guilty of an offence under
  subsection (1) or (2) if the person did not know, and in the exercise
  of reasonable diligence could not have known, of the untrue statement
  or omission.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Register of individuals with
  significant control

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, a register referred to in
  subsection 21.1(1) or an extract from it is not a report, return,
  notice or other document for the purposes of this section.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 250
- 2001, c. 14, s. 135(E)
- [2018, c. 27, s. 184](#1039122-1144116){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039122-1144116.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-250-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 250"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Offence

**[[251]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-251 .sectionLabel}** Every person who,
without reasonable cause, contravenes a provision of this Act or the
regulations for which no punishment is provided is guilty of an offence
punishable on summary conviction.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1974-75-76, c. 33, s. 244
- 1978-79, c. 9, s. 1(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Order to comply

- **[[252]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-252
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where a person is guilty of an
  offence under this Act or the regulations, any court in which
  proceedings in respect of the offence are taken may, in addition to
  any punishment it may impose, order that person to comply with the
  provisions of this Act or the regulations for the contravention of
  which the person has been convicted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limitation period

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A prosecution for an offence under this Act may be
  instituted at any time within but not later than two years after the
  time when the subject-matter of the complaint arose.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Civil remedy not affected

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} No civil remedy for an act or omission is suspended
  or affected by reason that the act or omission is an offence under
  this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 252
- 2001, c. 14, s. 135(E)
:::

## [PART XX.1]{.HLabel1}[Documents in Electronic or Other Form]{.HTitleText1} {#h-111974 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

**[[252.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-252.1 .sectionLabel}** The definitions in
this section apply in this Part.

[[electronic document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[electronic document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, except in section
    252.6, any form of representation of information or of concepts
    fixed in any medium in or by electronic, optical or other similar
    means and that can be read or perceived by a person or by any
    means. ([document électronique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

[[information system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

:   [[information system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a system used to
    generate, send, receive, store, or otherwise process an electronic
    document. ([système d'information]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 121
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application

**[[252.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-252.2 .sectionLabel}** This Part does not
apply to a notice, document or other information sent to or issued by
the Director pursuant to this Act or to any prescribed notice, document
or other information.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 121
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Use not mandatory

- **[[252.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-252.3
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Nothing in this Act or the
  regulations requires a person to create or provide an electronic
  document.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Consent and other requirements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite anything in this Part, a requirement under
  this Act or the regulations to provide a person with a notice,
  document or other information is not satisfied by the provision of an
  electronic document unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the addressee has consented, in the manner
    prescribed, and has designated an information system for the receipt
    of the electronic document; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the electronic document is provided to the
    designated information system, unless otherwise prescribed.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revocation of consent

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An addressee may revoke the consent referred to in
  paragraph (2)(a) in the manner prescribed.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 121
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Creation and provision of information

**[[252.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-252.4 .sectionLabel}** A requirement under
this Act or the regulations that a notice, document or other information
be created or provided, is satisfied by the creation or provision of an
electronic document if

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the by-laws or the articles of the corporation do not
  provide otherwise; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the regulations, if any, have been complied with.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 121
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Creation of information in writing

- **[[252.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-252.5
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A requirement under this Act or the
  regulations that a notice, document or other information be created in
  writing is satisfied by the creation of an electronic document if, in
  addition to the conditions in section 252.4,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information in the electronic document is
    accessible so as to be usable for subsequent reference; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the regulations pertaining to this subsection, if
    any, have been complied with.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Provision of information in writing

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A requirement under this Act or the regulations that
  a notice, document or other information be provided in writing is
  satisfied by the provision of an electronic document if, in addition
  to the conditions set out in section 252.4,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information in the electronic document is
    accessible by the addressee and capable of being retained by the
    addressee, so as to be usable for subsequent reference; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the regulations pertaining to this subsection, if
    any, have been complied with.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A requirement under this Act or the regulations for
  one or more copies of a document to be provided to a single addressee
  at the same time is satisfied by the provision of a single version of
  the electronic document.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registered mail

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A requirement under this Act or the regulations to
  provide a document by registered mail is not satisfied by the sending
  of an electronic document unless prescribed.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 121
- 2018, c. 8, s. 35(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-252.5-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 252.5"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Statutory declarations and affidavits

- **[[252.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-252.6
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A statutory declaration or an
  affidavit required under this Act or the regulations may be created or
  provided in an electronic document if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the person who makes the statutory declaration or
    affidavit signs it with his or her secure electronic signature;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the authorized person before whom the statutory
    declaration or affidavit is made signs it with his or her secure
    electronic signature; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the requirements of sections 252.3 to 252.5 are
    complied with.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, [[electronic
  document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} and [[secure electronic
  signature]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} have the same meaning as in subsection
  31(1) of the [Personal Information Protection and Electronic Documents
  Act](/eng/acts/P-8.6).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clarification

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of complying with paragraph (1)(c),
  the references to an [[electronic document]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} in
  sections 252.3 to 252.5 are to be read as references to an "electronic
  document" as defined in subsection 31(1) of the [Personal Information
  Protection and Electronic Documents Act](/eng/acts/P-8.6).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 121
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Signatures

**[[252.7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-252.7 .sectionLabel}** A requirement under
this Act or the regulations for a signature or for a document to be
executed, except with respect to a statutory declaration or an
affidavit, is satisfied if, in relation to an electronic document, the
prescribed requirements pertaining to this section, if any, are met and
if the signature results from the application by a person of a
technology or a process that permits the following to be proven:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the signature resulting from the use by a person of
  the technology or process is unique to the person;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the technology or process is used by a person to
  incorporate, attach or associate the person's signature to the
  electronic document; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the technology or process can be used to identify the
  person using the technology or process.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 121
:::

## [PART XXI]{.HLabel1}[General]{.HTitleText1} {#h-112036 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to directors and shareholders

- **[[253]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-253 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  notice or document required by this Act, the regulations, the articles
  or the by-laws to be sent to a shareholder or director of a
  corporation may be sent by prepaid mail addressed to, or may be
  delivered personally to,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the shareholder at the shareholder's latest address
    as shown in the records of the corporation or its transfer agent;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the director at the director's latest address as
    shown in the records of the corporation or in the last notice filed
    under section 106 or 113.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Effect of notice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A director named in a notice sent by a corporation to
  the Director under section 106 or 113 and filed by the Director is
  presumed for the purposes of this Act to be a director of the
  corporation referred to in the notice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed receipt

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A notice or document sent in accordance with
  subsection (1) to a shareholder or director of a corporation is deemed
  to be received at the time it would be delivered in the ordinary
  course of mail unless there are reasonable grounds for believing that
  the shareholder or director did not receive the notice or document at
  that time or at all.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Undelivered notices

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a corporation sends a notice or document to a
  shareholder in accordance with subsection (1) and the notice or
  document is returned on two consecutive occasions because the
  shareholder cannot be found, the corporation is not required to send
  any further notices or documents to the shareholder until the
  shareholder informs the corporation in writing of the shareholder's
  new address.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 253
- 2001, c. 14, ss. 122, 135(E)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to and service on a corporation

**[[254]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-254 .sectionLabel}** A notice or document
required to be sent to or served on a corporation may be sent by
registered mail to the registered office of the corporation shown in the
last notice filed under section 19 and, if so sent, is deemed to be
received or served at the time it would be delivered in the ordinary
course of mail unless there are reasonable grounds for believing that
the corporation did not receive the notice or document at that time or
at all.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1974-75-76, c. 33, s. 247
- 1978-79, c. 9, ss. 1(F), 79
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Waiver of notice

**[[255]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-255 .sectionLabel}** Where a notice or
document is required by this Act or the regulations to be sent, the
sending of the notice or document may be waived or the time for the
notice or document may be waived or abridged at any time with the
consent in writing of the person entitled thereto.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1974-75-76, c. 33, s. 248
- 1978-79, c. 9, ss. 1(F), 79
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of Director

- **[[256]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-256
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Where this Act requires or
  authorizes the Director to issue a certificate or to certify any fact,
  the certificate shall be signed by the Director or by a Deputy
  Director authorized under section 260.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Evidence

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Except in a proceeding under section 213 to dissolve
  a corporation, a certificate referred to in subsection (1) or a
  certified copy thereof, when introduced as evidence in any civil,
  criminal or administrative action or proceeding, is conclusive proof
  of the facts so certified without proof of the signature or official
  character of the person appearing to have signed the certificate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1974-75-76, c. 33, s. 249
- 1978-79, c. 9, s. 1(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate of corporation

- **[[257]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-257 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  certificate issued on behalf of a corporation stating any fact that is
  set out in the articles, the by-laws, a unanimous shareholder
  agreement, the minutes of the meetings of the directors, a committee
  of directors or the shareholders, or in a trust indenture or other
  contract to which the corporation is a party, may be signed by a
  director, an officer or a transfer agent of the corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} When introduced as evidence in any civil, criminal or
  administrative action or proceeding,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a fact stated in a certificate referred to in
    subsection (1),

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a certified extract from a securities register of a
    corporation, or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a certified copy of minutes or extract from minutes
    of a meeting of shareholders, directors or a committee of directors
    of a corporation,

  is, in the absence of evidence to the contrary, proof of the facts so
  certified without proof of the signature or official character of the
  person appearing to have signed the certificate.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Security certificate

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} An entry in a securities register of, or a security
  certificate issued by, a corporation is, in the absence of evidence to
  the contrary, proof that the person in whose name the security is
  registered is owner of the securities described in the register or in
  the certificate.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 257
- 2001, c. 14, s. 123(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies

**[[258]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-258 .sectionLabel}** Where a notice or
document is required to be sent to the Director under this Act, the
Director may accept a photostatic or photographic copy thereof.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1974-75-76, c. 33, s. 251
- 1978-79, c. 9, s. 1(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Content and form of documents

**[[258.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-258.1 .sectionLabel}** The Director may
establish the requirements for the content and fix the form, including
electronic or other forms, of notices, documents or other information
sent to or issued by the Director under this Act and, in so doing, the
Director may specify, among other things,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the notices, documents or other information that may
  be transmitted in electronic or other form;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the persons or classes of persons who may sign or
  transmit the notices, documents or other information;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the manner of signing, whether electronic or
  otherwise, or the actions that are to have the same effect as their
  signature; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the time and circumstances when electronic notices,
  documents or other information are to be considered to be sent or
  received, and the place where they are considered to have been sent or
  received.

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2018, c. 8, s. 36\]]{.Repealed}

::: HistoricalNote
- 1994, c. 24, s. 26
- 2001, c. 14, s. 124
- 2018, c. 8, s. 36
- [2023, c. 29, s. 8](#1426323-1438964){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1426323-1438964.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-258.1-20180501.html "Link to previous version of section 258.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption

**[[258.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-258.2 .sectionLabel}** In the prescribed
circumstances, the Director may, on any conditions that the Director
considers appropriate, exempt from the application of any provision of
this Act requiring notices or documents to be sent to the Director any
notices or documents or classes of notices or documents containing
information similar to that contained in notices or documents required
to be made public pursuant to any other Act of Parliament or to any Act
of the legislature of a province as the Director specifies.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1994, c. 24, s. 26
- 2001, c. 14, s. 124
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Proof required by Director

- **[[259]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-259 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Director may require that a document or a fact stated in a document or
  in any information required by this Act or the regulations to be sent
  to the Director shall be verified in accordance with subsection (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form of proof

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A document or fact required by this Act or by the
  Director to be verified may be verified by affidavit or by statutory
  declaration under the [Canada Evidence Act](/eng/acts/C-5) before any
  commissioner for oaths or for taking affidavits.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 259
- 2001, c. 14, s. 135(E)
- [2023, c. 29, s. 9](#1426336-1438970){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1426336-1438970.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-259-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 259"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Appointment of Director

**[[260]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-260 .sectionLabel}** The Minister may
appoint a Director and one or more Deputy Directors to carry out the
duties and exercise the powers of the Director under this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1974-75-76, c. 33, s. 253
- 1978-79, c. 9, s. 1(F)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Regulations

- **[[261]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-261 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Governor in Council may make regulations

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} providing for anything that by this Act is to be
    prescribed or provided for by the regulations;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} defining anything that, by this Act, is to be
    defined by regulation;

  - [(a.2)]{.lawlabel} prescribing what constitutes

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of paragraph 2.1(1)(b), direct
      influence, indirect influence or control in fact, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} for the purposes of paragraph 21.31(3)(c),
      direct influence or indirect influence;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} requiring the payment of a fee in respect of the
    receipt, examination, filing, issuance or copying of any document or
    other information, or in respect of any action that the Director is
    required or authorized to take under this Act, and prescribing the
    amount of the fee or the manner of determining the fee;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} respecting the payment of fees, including the time
    when and the manner in which the fees are to be paid, the additional
    fees that may be charged for the late payment of fees and the
    circumstances in which any fees previously paid may be refunded in
    whole or in part;

  - [(c.01)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the form of the register referred to
    in subsection 21.1(1) and the manner of preparing and maintaining
    it;

  - [(c.02)]{.lawlabel} respecting steps to be taken by a corporation
    for the purposes of subsection 21.1(2);

  - [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} prescribing, for the purposes of subsection
    137(1.1), a manner of determining the number of shares required for
    a person to be eligible to submit a proposal, including the time and
    manner of determining a value or percentage of the outstanding
    shares of the corporation;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} prescribing, for the purposes of paragraph
    137(5)(d), the minimum amount of support required in relation to the
    number of times the shareholder has submitted substantially the same
    proposal within the prescribed period;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} prescribing rules with respect to exemptions
    permitted by this Act;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} respecting the powers that may be granted by a
    shareholder in a form of proxy;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} prescribing any matter necessary for the purposes
    of the application of Part XX.1, including the time and
    circumstances when an electronic document is to be considered to
    have been provided or received and the place where it is considered
    to have been provided or received;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the manner of, and conditions for,
    participating in a meeting by means of a telephonic, electronic or
    other communication facility that permits all participants to
    communicate adequately with each other during the meeting; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} prescribing, for the purposes of subsection 141(3),
    the manner of, and conditions for, voting at a meeting of
    shareholders by means of a telephonic, electronic or other
    communication facility.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incorporation by reference

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The regulations may incorporate any material by
  reference regardless of its source and either as it exists on a
  particular date or as amended from time to time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incorporated material is not a
  regulation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Material does not become a regulation for the
  purposes of the [Statutory Instruments Act](/eng/acts/S-22) because it
  is incorporated by reference.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 261
- 1994, c. 24, s. 27
- 2001, c. 14, s. 125
- 2018, c. 8, s. 38
- [2018, c. 27, s. 185](#1039124-1144119){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1039124-1144119.html"
  role="button"}
- [2023, c. 29, s. 10](#1426340-1438972){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1426340-1438972.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-261-20190613.html "Link to previous version of section 261"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Payment of fees

**[[261.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-261.1 .sectionLabel}** A fee in respect of
the receipt or copying of any document or other information shall be
paid to the Director when it is received or copied, and a fee in respect
of the examination, filing or issuance of any document or other
information or in respect of any action that the Director is required or
authorized to take shall be paid to the Director before the document or
other information is examined, filed or issued or the action is taken.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 126
- 2018, c. 8, s. 39
- [2023, c. 29, s. 11](#1426344-1438977){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1426344-1438977.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-261.1-20180501.html "Link to previous version of section 261.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
[[statement]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[262]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-262 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, [[statement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a statement of
  intent to dissolve and a statement of revocation of intent to dissolve
  referred to in section 211.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sending of articles and statements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If this Act requires that articles or a statement
  relating to a corporation be sent to the Director, on receiving the
  articles or statement in the form that the Director fixes, any other
  required documents and the required fees, the Director shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} record the date of receipt;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} issue the appropriate certificate;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} send the certificate, or a copy, image or
    photographic, electronic or other reproduction of the certificate,
    to the corporation or its agent or mandatary; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} publish a notice of the issuance of the certificate
    in a publication generally available to the public.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date of certificate

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A certificate referred to in subsection (2) issued by
  the Director may be dated as of the day the Director receives the
  articles, statement or court order pursuant to which the certificate
  is issued or as of any later day specified by the court or person who
  signed the articles or statement.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 1994, c. 24, s. 28\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date of certificate

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Notwithstanding subsection (3), a certificate of
  discontinuance may be dated as of the day on which the corporation
  amalgamates pursuant to another Act or is continued.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 262
- 1994, c. 24, s. 28
- 2001, c. 14, ss. 127, 135(E)
- 2011, c. 21, s. 71(E)
- 2018, c. 8, s. 40
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-262-20111129.html "Link to previous version of section 262"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Signature

- **[[262.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-262.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A signature required on a
  certificate issued by the Director under this Act may be printed or
  otherwise mechanically reproduced on the certificate or may be in
  accordance with the regulations made under paragraph 261(1)(c.1).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2018, c. 8, s. 41\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Execution of documents

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Any articles, notice, resolution, requisition,
  statement or other document required or permitted to be executed or
  signed by more than one individual for the purposes of this Act may be
  executed or signed in several documents of like form, each of which is
  executed or signed by one or more of the individuals. The documents,
  when duly executed or signed by all individuals required or permitted,
  as the case may be, to do so, shall be deemed to constitute one
  document for the purposes of this Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1994, c. 24, s. 29
- 2001, c. 14, s. 128
- 2018, c. 8, s. 41
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-262.1-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 262.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Publication

**[[262.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-262.2 .sectionLabel}** The Director must
publish, in a publication generally available to the public, a notice of
any decision made by the Director granting an application made under
subsection 2(6), 10(2), 21.303(3), 82(3) or 151(1), section 156 or
subsection 171(2) or 187(11).

::: HistoricalNote
- 2018, c. 8, s. 42
- [2023, c. 29, s. 12](#1426348-1438980){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1426348-1438980.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-262.2-20180501.html "Link to previous version of section 262.2"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Annual return

**[[263]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-263 .sectionLabel}** Every corporation shall
send to the Director an annual return in the form and within the period
that the Director fixes.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 263
- 2001, c. 14, s. 129
- 2018, c. 8, s. 42
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-263-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 263"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Certificate

- **[[263.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-263.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Director may provide any person
  with a certificate stating that a corporation

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} has sent to the Director a document or other
    information required to be sent under this Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} has paid all required fees; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} exists as of a certain date.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Director may refuse to issue
  certificate of existence

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the Director may refuse to
  issue a certificate described in paragraph (1)(c) if the Director has
  knowledge that the corporation is in default of sending a document or
  other information required to be sent under this Act or is in default
  of paying a required fee.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 129
- [2023, c. 29, s. 13](#1426352-1438983){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1426352-1438983.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-263.1-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 263.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Alteration

**[[264]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-264 .sectionLabel}** The Director may alter
a notice or document, other than an affidavit or statutory declaration,
if authorized by the person who sent the document or by that person's
representative.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 264
- 2001, c. 14, s. 135(E)
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Corrections at request of Director

- **[[265]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-265 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  there is an error in articles, a notice, a certificate or other
  document, the directors or shareholders of the corporation shall, on
  the request of the Director, pass the resolutions and send to the
  Director the documents required to comply with this Act, and take such
  other steps as the Director may reasonably require so that the
  Director may correct the document.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No prejudice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Before proceeding under subsection (1), the Director
  must be satisfied that the correction would not prejudice any of the
  shareholders or creditors of the corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Corrections at the request of the
  corporation

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Director may, at the request of the corporation
  or of any other interested person, accept a correction to any of the
  documents referred to in subsection (1) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the correction is approved by the directors of the
    corporation, unless the error is obvious or was made by the
    Director; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Director is satisfied that the correction would
    not prejudice any of the shareholders or creditors of the
    corporation and that the correction reflects the original intention
    of the corporation or the incorporators, as the case may be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to court

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If, in the view of the Director, of the corporation
  or of any interested person who wishes a correction, a correction to
  any of the documents referred to in subsection (1) would prejudice any
  of the shareholders or creditors of a corporation, the Director, the
  corporation or the person, as the case may be, may apply to the court
  for an order that the document be corrected and for an order
  determining the rights of the shareholders or creditors.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to Director

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} An applicant under subsection (4) shall give the
  Director notice of the application, and the Director is entitled to
  appear and to be heard in person or by counsel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Director may require surrender of
  document

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Director may demand the surrender of the original
  document, and may issue a corrected certificate or file the corrected
  articles, notice or other document.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Date of corrected document

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} A corrected document shall bear the date of the
  document it replaces unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the correction is made with respect to the date of
    the document, in which case the document shall bear the corrected
    date; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the court decides otherwise.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} If a corrected certificate materially amends the
  terms of the original certificate, the Director shall without delay
  give notice of the correction in a publication generally available to
  the public.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 265
- 2001, c. 14, s. 130
- 2018, c. 8, s. 43(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-265-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 265"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation of articles by Director

- **[[265.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-265.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In the prescribed circumstances, the
  Director may cancel the articles and related certificate of a
  corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No prejudice

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Before proceeding under subsection (1), the Director
  must be satisfied that the cancellation would not prejudice any of the
  shareholders or creditors of the corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Request to Director to cancel articles

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the prescribed circumstances, the Director may, at
  the request of a corporation or of any other interested person, cancel
  the articles and related certificate of the corporation if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the cancellation is approved by the directors of
    the corporation; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the Director is satisfied that the cancellation
    would not prejudice any of the shareholders or creditors of the
    corporation and that the cancellation reflects the original
    intention of the corporation or the incorporators, as the case may
    be.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application to court

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If, in the view of the Director, of the corporation
  or of any interested person who wishes a cancellation, a cancellation
  of articles and a related certificate would prejudice any of the
  shareholders or creditors of a corporation, the Director, the
  corporation or the person, as the case may be, may apply to the court
  for an order that the articles and certificate be cancelled and for an
  order determining the rights of the shareholders or creditors.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to Director

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} An applicant under subsection (4) shall give the
  Director notice of the application, and the Director is entitled to
  appear and to be heard in person or by counsel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Return of certificate

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Director may demand the surrender of a cancelled
  certificate.

::: HistoricalNote
- 2001, c. 14, s. 130
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Examination

- **[[266]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-266 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who has paid the required fee is entitled during usual business
  hours to examine and make copies of or take extracts from a document
  or other information --- except any information sent under section
  21.21, an application referred to in subsection 21.303(3) and any
  related document and a report sent to the Director under subsection
  230(2) --- that is required to be sent to the Director under this Act
  or that was required to be sent to a person performing a similar
  function under prior legislation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Copies or extracts

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Director shall, on request, provide any person
  with a copy, extract, certified copy or certified extract of a
  document that may be examined under subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 266
- 2001, c. 14, s. 130
- [2018, c. 8, s. 44](#1037675-1373837){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037675-1373837.html"
  role="button"}
- [2022, c. 10, s. 433](#1358030-1438987){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1358030-1438987.html"
  role="button"}
- [2023, c. 29, s. 14](#1426361-1438988){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1426361-1438988.html"
  role="button"}
- [2023, c. 29, s. 20](#1426391-1438989){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1426391-1438989.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-266-20220831.html "Link to previous version of section 266"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Information not to be made available

- **[[266.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-266.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Director shall not make
  available any information that could reasonably be expected to reveal
  the identity of a person who, on their own initiative, provides
  information relating to the commission or potential commission of a
  wrongdoing, or any information provided by the person in relation to
  the commission or potential commission, unless the person providing
  the information consents to its being made available.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- investigations

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), the Director may provide
  information referred to in that subsection to an investigative body
  referred to in subsection 21.31(2), the Financial Transactions and
  Reports Analysis Centre of Canada or any prescribed entity.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
  [[wrongdoing]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[wrongdoing]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}
  includes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a contravention of any provision of this Act or the
    regulations;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the formation of a corporation for a fraudulent or
    unlawful purpose; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any fraudulent or dishonest actions of persons
    concerned with the formation, business or affairs of a corporation.

::: HistoricalNote
- [2023, c. 29, s. 15](#1426365-1439006){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1426365-1439006.html"
  role="button"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Director's obligation to keep documents

- **[[267]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-267
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Documents that are received and
  accepted by the Director under this Act or that were received and
  accepted by a person performing a similar function under prior
  legislation shall be kept by the Director, in any form.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Obligation to furnish

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If documents are kept by the Director otherwise than
  in written form,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Director shall furnish any copy required to be
    furnished under subsection 266(2) in intelligible form; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a report reproduced from those documents, if it is
    certified by the Director, is admissible in evidence to the same
    extent as the original documents would have been.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time period for keeping and producing
  documents

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Director is not required to keep or produce any
  document or class of documents --- other than a certificate and any
  attached articles or statement received under section 262 and other
  prescribed documents or prescribed class of documents --- after the
  end of the period prescribed for the keeping or production of the
  document or class of documents.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 267
- 1994, c. 24, s. 30
- 2001, c. 14, s. 131
- [2018, c. 8, s. 45](#1037696-1373844){.wb-lbx
  aria-controls="centred-popup" ajax-after="1037696-1373844.html"
  role="button"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-267-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 267"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Form of publication

**[[267.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-267.1 .sectionLabel}** Information or
notices required by this Act to be summarized in a publication generally
available to the public or published by the Director may be made
available to the public or published by any system of mechanical or
electronic data processing or by any other information storage device
that is capable of reproducing any required information or notice in
intelligible form within a reasonable time.

::: HistoricalNote
- 1994, c. 24, s. 31
- 2001, c. 14, s. 132
- 2018, c. 8, s. 46(F)
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-267.1-20030101.html "Link to previous version of section 267.1"){.PITLink}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
[[charter]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

- **[[268]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-268 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  this section, [[charter]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an act of incorporation and any amendments thereto;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} letters patent of incorporation and any letters
    patent supplementary thereto.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendment of charter --- special Act

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} In connection with a continuance under this Act, the
  shareholders of a body corporate incorporated or continued by or under
  a special Act of Parliament who are entitled to vote at annual
  meetings of shareholders may, despite the charter of the body
  corporate,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} by special resolution, authorize the directors of
    the body corporate to apply under section 187 for a certificate of
    continuance; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} by the same resolution, make any amendment to the
    charter of the body corporate that a corporation incorporated under
    this Act may make to its articles.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Amendment of charter --- other Act

  [(2.1)]{.lawlabel} In connection with a continuance under this Act,
  the shareholders of a body corporate incorporated or continued by or
  under an Act of Parliament, other than this Act or a special Act, who
  are entitled to vote at annual meetings of shareholders may, subject
  to any other Act of Parliament or the charter of the body corporate,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} by special resolution, authorize the directors of
    the body corporate to apply under section 187 for a certificate of
    continuance; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} by the same resolution, make any amendment to the
    charter of the body corporate that a corporation incorporated under
    this Act may make to its articles.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Change of class rights

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsections (2) and (2.1), the shareholders
  of a body corporate may not, by a special resolution under any of
