  those subsections, make any change of the nature referred to in
  subsection 176(1) that affects a class or series of shares, unless

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the charter of the body corporate otherwise
    provides in respect of an amendment of the nature referred to in
    paragraph 176(1)(a), (b) or (e); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the holders of the class or series of shares
    approve the change in accordance with section 176.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorizing continuance

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (6), the directors of a body
  corporate incorporated or continued by or under a special Act of
  Parliament may, despite the charter of the body corporate, apply under
  section 187 for a certificate of continuance if the articles of
  continuance do not make any amendment to the charter of the body
  corporate other than an amendment required to conform to this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Authorizing continuance

  [(4.1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (6), the directors of a body
  corporate incorporated or continued by or under an Act of Parliament,
  other than this Act or a special Act, may, subject to any other Act of
  Parliament or the charter of the body corporate, apply under section
  187 for a certificate of continuance if the articles of continuance do
  not make any amendment to the charter of the body corporate other than
  an amendment required to conform to this Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Financial institutions

  [(4.2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, every body
  corporate that is incorporated or continued under an Act of Parliament
  and to which the [Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01), the [Cooperative Credit
  Associations Act](/eng/acts/C-41.01), the [Insurance Companies
  Act](/eng/acts/I-11.8) or the [Trust and Loan Companies
  Act](/eng/acts/T-19.8) applies is deemed to be incorporated or
  continued by or under an Act of Parliament other than this Act or a
  special Act.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No dissent

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} A shareholder is not entitled to dissent under
  section 190 in respect of an amendment made under subsection (2),
  (2.1), (3), (4) or (4.1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Discretionary continuance

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Governor in Council may, by order, require that a
  body corporate incorporated by or under an Act of Parliament to which
  the [Canada Not-for-profit Corporations Act](/eng/acts/C-7.75) does
  not apply, apply for a certificate of continuance under section 187
  within any period that may be prescribed except for the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a bank;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} an association to which the [Cooperative Credit
    Associations Act](/eng/acts/C-41.01) applies;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a company or society to which the [Insurance
    Companies Act](/eng/acts/I-11.8) applies; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a company to which the [Trust and Loan Companies
    Act](/eng/acts/T-19.8) applies.

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, 2009, c. 23, s. 311\]]{.Repealed}

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Fees

  [(8)]{.lawlabel} A body corporate that obtains a certificate of
  continuance under this section is not required to pay any fees
  otherwise payable under this Act in respect of such continuance.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Special Act no longer applicable

  [(8.1)]{.lawlabel} On the continuance of a body corporate incorporated
  by a special Act of Parliament as a corporation under this Act, the
  special Act ceases to apply to the corporation.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Idem

  [(9)]{.lawlabel} A body corporate referred to in subsection (6) that
  does not make an application to obtain a certificate of continuance
  within the period prescribed is dissolved on the expiration of that
  period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Continuance prohibited

  [(10)]{.lawlabel} A body corporate to which the [Canada Not-for-profit
  Corporations Act](/eng/acts/C-7.75) applies or any similar body
  corporate incorporated otherwise than by or under an Act of Parliament
  may not apply for a certificate of continuance under section 187.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception for railway companies

  [(11)]{.lawlabel} A body corporate that is incorporated by or under a
  Special Act, as defined in section 87 of the [Canada Transportation
  Act](/eng/acts/C-10.4), may apply for a certificate of continuance
  under section 187.

::: HistoricalNote
- R.S., 1985, c. C-44, s. 268
- 1991, c. 45, s. 556, c. 46, s. 597, c. 47, s. 724
- 1992, c. 1, ss. 142, 160(F)
- 1994, c. 24, s. 32
- 1996, c. 10, s. 213
- 2001, c. 14, s. 133
- 2007, c. 6, s. 401
- 2009, c. 23, ss. 311, 345, 346
:::

    (production, cultivation, propagation and harvesting);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} subsection 13(1) (possession, etc., for use in
    production or distribution of illicit cannabis);

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} subsection 14(1) (use of young person).

- **9** An offence under any Act of Parliament or law of the legislature
  of a province that provides for the imposition of a tax or duty for
  which the maximum term of imprisonment is five years or more.

## RELATED PROVISIONS {#related-provisions .scheduleTitleText}

- --- R.S., 1985, c. 27 (2nd Supp.), s. 11

  - ###### Transitional: proceedings {#transitional-proceedings .MarginalNote}

    [11]{.lawlabel} Proceedings to which any of the provisions amended
    by the schedule apply that were commenced before the coming into
    force of section 10 shall be continued in accordance with those
    amended provisions without any further formality.

- --- 1990, c. 17, s. 45(1)

  - ###### Transitional: proceedings {#transitional-proceedings-1 .MarginalNote}

    - [45]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every proceeding commenced before
      the coming into force of this subsection and in respect of which
      any provision amended by this Act applies shall be taken up and
      continued under and in conformity with that amended provision
      without any further formality.

- --- 1994, c. 21, s. 125(2)

  - - [125]{.lawlabel} [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) applies after
      1988.

- --- 1994, c. 24, s. 33

  - ###### Review of [Canada Business Corporations Act](/eng/acts/C-44) {#review-of-canada-business-corporations-act .MarginalNote}

    - [33]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Within three years after the day
      on which this Act is assented to, the Minister shall cause to be
      laid before both Houses of Parliament a report on the provisions
      and operation of the [Canada Business Corporations
      Act](/eng/acts/C-44), including any recommendations for amendments
      to that Act.

    - ###### Reference to parliamentary committee {#reference-to-parliamentary-committee .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} The report stands referred to the committee of
      the House of Commons, or of both Houses of Parliament, that is
      designated or established for that purpose, which shall

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} as soon as possible thereafter review the
        report and undertake a comprehensive review of the provisions
        and operation of the [Canada Business Corporations
        Act](/eng/acts/C-44); and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} report to the House of Commons, or to both
        Houses of Parliament, within one year after the laying of the
        report of the Minister or any further time that the House of
        Commons, or both Houses of Parliament, may authorize.

- --- 1996, c. 10, s. 214

  - ###### Previous continuance of railway companies {#previous-continuance-of-railway-companies .MarginalNote}

    [214]{.lawlabel} No continuance of a railway company granted under
    section 187 of the [Canada Business Corporations
    Act](/eng/acts/C-44) before section 212 comes into force is invalid
    because the company was incorporated by or under an Act of
    Parliament.

- --- 1998, c. 30, s. 10

  - ###### Transitional --- proceedings {#transitional-proceedings-2 .MarginalNote}

    [10]{.lawlabel} Every proceeding commenced before the coming into
    force of this section and in respect of which any provision amended
    by sections 12 to 16 applies shall be taken up and continued under
    and in conformity with that amended provision without any further
    formality.

- --- 2001, c. 14, s. 136

  - ###### Review of [Canada Business Corporations Act](/eng/acts/C-44) {#review-of-canada-business-corporations-act-1 .MarginalNote}

    [136]{.lawlabel} A committee of the Senate, of the House of Commons
    or of both Houses of Parliament that is designated or established
    for the purpose shall, within five years after the coming into force
    of this section, and within every ten years thereafter, undertake a
    review of the provisions and operations of the [Canada Business
    Corporations Act](/eng/acts/C-44), and shall, within a reasonable
    period thereafter, cause to be laid before each House of Parliament
    a report thereon.

- --- 2001, c. 14, s. 233

  - [233]{.lawlabel} Part XIX.1 of the [Canada Business Corporations
    Act](/eng/acts/C-44), as enacted by section 115 of this Act, does
    not apply to any proceeding commenced before the coming into force
    of that section.

- --- 2009, c. 23, s. 297(6)

  - ###### Time limit for continuance {#time-limit-for-continuance .MarginalNote}

    - [297]{.lawlabel} [(6)]{.lawlabel} Despite any provision of the
      [Canada Corporations Act](/eng/acts/C-1.8), a body corporate
      referred to in subsection (2) that does not apply for a
      certificate of continuance under section 187 of the [Canada
      Business Corporations Act](/eng/acts/C-44) within six months after
      the day on which this subsection comes into force is dissolved on
      the expiry of that period.

- --- 2011, c. 25, s. 42

  - ###### Submission to Minister {#submission-to-minister .MarginalNote}

    - [42]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} The Corporation must submit an
      application for continuance under one of the following Acts for
      the Minister's approval:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the [Canada Business Corporations
        Act](/eng/acts/C-44);

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the [Canada Cooperatives Act](/eng/acts/C-1.7);
        or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the [Canada Not-for-profit Corporations
        Act](/eng/acts/C-7.75).

    - ###### Deadline {#deadline .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} The application must be submitted to the Minister
      within four years, or any shorter period specified by the
      Minister, after the day on which this Part comes into force.

    - ###### Submission to applicable authorities {#submission-to-applicable-authorities .MarginalNote}

      [(3)]{.lawlabel} Once the application has been approved by the
      Minister, the Corporation must submit the application to the
      applicable authorities under the relevant Act.

    - ###### Application not invalid {#application-not-invalid .MarginalNote}

      [(4)]{.lawlabel} The application is not invalid solely because the
      Corporation is incorporated by an Act of Parliament.

- --- 2012, c. 19, s. 210

  - ###### Status of PPP Canada Inc. {#status-of-ppp-canada-inc. .MarginalNote}

    [210]{.lawlabel} Except as provided in this Division, PPP Canada
    Inc., incorporated under the [Canada Business Corporations
    Act](/eng/acts/C-44), is not an agent of Her Majesty in right of
    Canada.

- --- 2018, c. 8, s. 107.1

  - ###### Review {#review .MarginalNote}

    - [107.1]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} On the fifth anniversary of
      the day on which section 24 of this Act comes into force, the
      provisions under Part XIV.1 of the [Canada Business Corporations
      Act](/eng/acts/C-44) are to be referred to a committee of the
      Senate, of the House of Commons or of both Houses of Parliament
      that may be designated or established for the purpose of reviewing
      those provisions.

    - ###### Report to Parliament {#report-to-parliament .MarginalNote}

      [(2)]{.lawlabel} The committee referred to in subsection (1)
      shall, within a reasonable time after a review is completed,
      submit a report on that review to the Senate, the House of Commons
      or both Houses of Parliament, as the case may be.
:::
::::

:::: section
::: ScheduleNIF
## AMENDMENTS NOT IN FORCE {#amendments-not-in-force .scheduleTitleText}

- --- 2018, c. 8, s. 17

  - ###### [1992, c. 1, s. 54]{.HistoricalNote} {#c.-1-s.-54 .MarginalNote}

    [17]{.lawlabel} Subsections 150(1) to (2) of the Act are replaced by
    the following:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - ###### Soliciting proxies {#soliciting-proxies .MarginalNote}

      - [150]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} A person shall not solicit
        proxies unless a proxy circular, in the prescribed form, is made
        available in the prescribed manner to the auditor of the
        corporation, to each shareholder whose proxy is solicited, to
        each director and, in the case set out in paragraph (b), to the
        corporation as follows:

        - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of solicitation by or on behalf
          of the management of a corporation, a management proxy
          circular, either as an appendix to or as a separate document
          accompanying the notice of the meeting; or

        - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of any other solicitation, a
          dissident's proxy circular stating the purposes of the
          solicitation.

      - ###### Exception --- solicitation to 15 or fewer shareholders {#exception-solicitation-to-15-or-fewer-shareholders .MarginalNote}

        [(1.1)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), a person may solicit
        proxies, other than by or on behalf of the management of the
        corporation, without making available a dissident's proxy
        circular, if the total number of shareholders whose proxies are
        solicited is 15 or fewer, two or more joint holders being
        counted as one shareholder.

      - ###### Exception --- solicitation by public broadcast {#exception-solicitation-by-public-broadcast .MarginalNote}

        [(1.2)]{.lawlabel} Despite subsection (1), a person may solicit
        proxies, other than by or on behalf of the management of the
        corporation, without making available a dissident's proxy
        circular if the solicitation is, in the prescribed
        circumstances, conveyed by public broadcast, speech or
        publication.

      - ###### Copy to Director {#copy-to-director .MarginalNote}

        [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person required to make a management proxy
        circular or dissident's proxy circular available shall send
        concurrently a copy of it to the Director together with a
        statement in prescribed form, the form of proxy, any other
        documents for use in connection with the meeting and, in the
        case of a management proxy circular, a copy of the notice of
        meeting.
    :::
    ::::

- --- 2018, c. 8, s. 19

  - ###### [2001, c. 14, s. 72]{.HistoricalNote} {#c.-14-s.-72 .MarginalNote}

    [19]{.lawlabel} Subsection 153(1) of the Act is replaced by the
    following:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - ###### Duty of intermediary {#duty-of-intermediary .MarginalNote}

      - [153]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Shares of a corporation that
        are registered in the name of an intermediary or their nominee
        and not beneficially owned by the intermediary must not be voted
        unless the intermediary, without delay after receipt of the
        prescribed documents, sends a copy of those documents to the
        beneficial owner and, except when the intermediary has received
        written voting instructions from the beneficial owner, a written
        request for such instructions.
    :::
    ::::

- --- 2018, c. 8, s. 22

  - ###### [2001, c. 14, s. 135 (Sch., s. 51)(E)]{.HistoricalNote} {#c.-14-s.-135-sch.-s.-51e .MarginalNote}

    [22]{.lawlabel} Subsection 159(1) of the Act is replaced by the
    following:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - ###### Copies to shareholders {#copies-to-shareholders .MarginalNote}

      - [159]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} A corporation shall send,
        within a prescribed period, a copy of the prescribed documents
        relating to financial disclosure to the prescribed shareholders
        and other prescribed persons.
    :::
    ::::

- --- 2018, c. 8, s. 37

  - [37]{.lawlabel} The Act is amended by adding the following after
    section 258.2:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - ###### Exemption {#exemption .MarginalNote}

      [258.3]{.lawlabel} The Director may, in the prescribed
      circumstances and on any conditions that the Director considers
      appropriate, exempt any corporation or any other person from a
      requirement set out in subsection 135(1), section 149 or
      subsection 150(1), 153(1) or 159(1) to send any notices or other
      documents, or classes of notices or other documents.
    :::
    ::::

- --- 2019, c. 29, s. 142

  - [142]{.lawlabel} The Act is amended by adding the following after
    section 125:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - ###### Development of an approach on remuneration {#development-of-an-approach-on-remuneration .MarginalNote}

      [125.1]{.lawlabel} A prescribed corporation shall develop an
      approach with respect to the remuneration of the directors and
      employees of the corporation who are "members of senior
      management" as defined by regulation.
    :::
    ::::

- --- 2019, c. 29, s. 143, as amended by 2019, c. 29, s. 151(3)

  - - [143]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Section 172.1 of the Act is
      replaced by the following:

      :::: section
      ::: AmendedText
      - ###### Diversity in corporations {#diversity-in-corporations .MarginalNote}

        [172.1]{.lawlabel} The directors of a prescribed corporation
        shall place before the shareholders, at every annual meeting,
        the prescribed information respecting diversity among the
        directors and "members of senior management" as defined by
        regulation.

      - ###### Information to shareholders {#information-to-shareholders .MarginalNote}

        - [172.2]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} The corporation shall
          provide the information referred to in section 172.1 to each
          shareholder, except to a shareholder who has informed the
          corporation in writing that they do not want to receive that
          information, by sending the information along with the notice
          referred to in subsection 135(1) or by making the information
          available along with a proxy circular referred to in
          subsection 150(1).

        - ###### Information to Director {#information-to-director .MarginalNote}

          [(2)]{.lawlabel} The corporation shall concurrently send the
          information referred to in section 172.1 to the Director.
      :::
      ::::

    - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The heading before section 172.1 of the Act is
      replaced by the following:

      :::: section
      ::: AmendedText
      ### [Disclosure Relating to Diversity, Well-being and Remuneration]{.HTitleText1} {#disclosure-relating-to-diversity-well-being-and-remuneration .SchedHeadL1}
      :::
      ::::

    - [(3)]{.lawlabel} Section 172.2 of the Act is replaced by the
      following:

      :::: section
      ::: AmendedText
      - ###### Information respecting well-being {#information-respecting-well-being .MarginalNote}

        [172.2]{.lawlabel} The directors of a prescribed corporation
        shall place before the shareholders, at every annual meeting,
        the prescribed information respecting the well-being of
        employees, retirees and pensioners.

      - ###### Recovery of benefits {#recovery-of-benefits .MarginalNote}

        [172.3]{.lawlabel} The directors of a prescribed corporation
        shall place before the shareholders, at every annual meeting,
        the prescribed information respecting the recovery of incentive
        benefits or other benefits, which is included in the
        remuneration referred to in section 125, paid to directors and
        employees of the corporation who are "members of senior
        management" as defined by regulation.

      - ###### Approach on remuneration {#approach-on-remuneration .MarginalNote}

        - [172.4]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} The directors of a
          prescribed corporation shall place before the shareholders, at
          every annual meeting, the approach with respect to
          remuneration referred to in section 125.1.

        - ###### Non-binding vote {#non-binding-vote .MarginalNote}

          [(2)]{.lawlabel} The shareholders are to vote on the approach
          placed before them by the directors under subsection (1). The
          results are not binding on the corporation.

        - ###### Disclosure of results {#disclosure-of-results .MarginalNote}

          [(3)]{.lawlabel} The corporation shall disclose the results of
          the vote to the shareholders.

      - ###### Information to shareholders {#information-to-shareholders-1 .MarginalNote}

        - [172.5]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} The corporation shall
          provide the information referred to in sections 172.1 to 172.4
          to each shareholder, except to a shareholder who has informed
          the corporation in writing that they do not want to receive
          that information, by sending the information along with the
          notice referred to in subsection 135(1) or by making the
          information available along with a proxy circular referred to
          in subsection 150(1).

        - ###### Information to Director {#information-to-director-1 .MarginalNote}

          [(2)]{.lawlabel} The corporation shall concurrently send the
          information referred to in sections 172.1 to 172.4 to the
          Director.
      :::
      ::::

- --- 2019, c. 29, s. 144

  - [144]{.lawlabel} Subsection 261(1) of the Act is amended by striking
    out "and" at the end of paragraph (h) and by adding the following
    after paragraph (i):

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - [(j)]{.lawlabel} defining, for the purposes of section 172.2, the
      expressions "retirees" and "pensioners"; and

    - [(k)]{.lawlabel} prescribing the time and manner in which the
      results of the vote referred to in subsection 172.4(3) are to be
      disclosed to the shareholders.
    :::
    ::::
:::
